## Cross-border Movement of Hazardous Waste and Hazardous Recyclable Material Regulations {#cross-border-movement-of-hazardous-waste-and-hazardous-recyclable-material-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2021-25
:::

[CANADA SHIPPING ACT, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)

[CANADIAN ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION ACT, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)

[ENVIRONMENTAL VIOLATIONS ADMINISTRATIVE MONETARY PENALTIES
ACT](/eng/acts/E-12.5)

[IMPACT ASSESSMENT ACT](/eng/acts/I-2.75)

Registration 2021-02-26

Cross-border Movement of Hazardous Waste and Hazardous Recyclable
Material Regulations

P.C. 2021-93 2021-02-26
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, pursuant to subsection 332(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25641){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25641-ID0EICBA}
of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644-ID0EFCBA},
the Minister of the Environment published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I, on December 15, 2018 a copy
of the proposed Cross-border Movement of Hazardous Waste and Hazardous
Recyclable Material Regulations, substantially in the annexed form, and
persons were given an opportunity to file comments with respect to the
proposed Regulations or to file a notice of objection requesting that a
board of review be established and stating the reasons for the
objection;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25641-ID0EICBA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25641}S.C.
  2004, c. 15, s. 31
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644-ID0EFCBA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644}S.C.
  1999, c. 33
  :::

Therefore, His Excellency the Administrator of the Government of Canada
in Council, on the recommendation of the Minister of the Environment and
the Minister of Health with respect to the provisions of the annexed
Regulations other than section 82, and on the recommendation of the
Minister of Transport with respect to that section 82, makes the annexed
Cross-border Movement of Hazardous Waste and Hazardous Recyclable
Material Regulations pursuant to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 93(1) and 102(1) and sections 191 and
  286.1[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25646){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25646-ID0EEBDAA}
  of the Canadian Environmental Protection Act (1999)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25644-ID0EBBDAA};

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25646-ID0EEBDAA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25646}S.C.
    2009, c. 14, s. 80
    :::

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 120(1)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25647){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25647-ID0EECCAA}
  of the [Canada Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25648){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25648-ID0EBCCAA};

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25647-ID0EECCAA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25647}S.C.
    2018, c. 27, s. 694
    :::

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^e^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25648-ID0EBCCAA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25648}S.C.
    2001, c. 26
    :::

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 109(b) of the [Impact Assessment
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^f^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25649){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25649-ID0EBBBAA};
  and

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^f^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25649-ID0EBBBAA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_25649}S.C.
    2019, c. 28, s. 1
    :::

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 5(1) of the [Environmental Violations
  Administrative Monetary Penalties Act](/eng/acts/E-12.5)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^g^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_27958){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_27958-ID0EBBAAA}.

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^g^](#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_27958-ID0EBBAAA){#fn_81000-2-1177_hq_27958}S.C.
    2009, c. 14, s. 126
    :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1286352 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in these Regulations.

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canadian Environmental
    Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[authorized carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, as the case may
    be,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a carrier that, in accordance with the [Canada
      Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15) and the regulations under
      that Act, transports --- or is to transport --- hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material by means of a boat, ship or craft
      designed, used or capable of being used solely or partly for
      navigation in, on, through or immediately above water; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a carrier that transports --- or is to transport
      --- hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in accordance
      with the requirements of the jurisdiction in which the waste or
      recyclable material is transported. ([transporteur
      agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[authorized facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[authorized facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility that is
    authorized by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which it is
    located to dispose of hazardous waste by means of a disposal
    operation set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 or to recycle
    hazardous recyclable material by means of a recycling operation set
    out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1. ([installation
    agréée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Canada-USA Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canada-USA Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Agreement
    Between the Government of Canada and the Government of the United
    States of America Concerning the Transboundary Movement of Hazardous
    Waste, which entered into force on November 8, 1986, as amended from
    time to time. ([accord Canada -- États-Unis]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[CAS Registry Number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CAS Registry Number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the identification
    number assigned to a substance by the Chemical Abstracts Service
    Division of the American Chemical Society. ([numéro d'enregistrement
    CAS]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[competent authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[competent authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in the case of a
    country that is a party to the Convention or is subject to OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, the authority that is designated as the
    competent authority by that country under the Convention or under
    OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, as the case may be, and, in the case
    of the United States, the United States Environmental Protection
    Agency. ([autorité compétente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[consignee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[consignee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that receives or is
    to receive delivery from a consignor of a shipment that contains
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material at a site that they
    own, operate or otherwise control. ([destinataire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[consignor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[consignor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that, on their own
    behalf or on behalf of another person under an agreement or
    arrangement with that person, sends or is to send a shipment that
    contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material from a
    site that is located in a province to a site that is located in
    another province and that is owned, operated or otherwise controlled
    by a consignee. ([expéditeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a contract --- or, if all of
    the parties are the same legal entity, an arrangement among those
    parties --- that is in writing and is signed and, if parties are the
    same legal entity that is doing business in both Canada and another
    country, is signed by the representatives of that entity in both
    countries. ([contrat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Basel Convention on the
    Control of Transboundary Movements of Hazardous Wastes and Their
    Disposal, which entered into force on May 5,
    1992. ([Convention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[environmentally hazardous constituent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[environmentally hazardous constituent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    a constituent that is contained in a leachate and is set out in
    column 3 of Schedule 2 in a concentration that is determined to be
    equal to or greater than the concentration set out in column 4 of
    that Schedule in accordance with Method 1311: Toxicity
    Characteristic Leaching Procedure, as set out in Test Methods for
    Evaluating Solid Waste, Physical/Chemical Methods, SW-846, Third
    Edition, published by the United States Environmental Protection
    Agency. ([constituant dangereux pour
    l'environnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[final disposal operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[final disposal operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a disposal
    operation set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 that bears one
    of the disposal codes D1 to D12, DC1 and DC2 set out in column 1 of
    that Part. ([opération finale d'élimination]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[final recycling operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[final recycling operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recycling
    operation set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1 that bears one
    of the recycling codes R1 to R11, RC1 and RC2 set out in column 1 of
    that Part. ([opération finale de recyclage]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[foreign exporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign exporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, including a
    government entity, in a foreign country that exports hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material from that country for import into,
    or conveyance in transit through, Canada. ([exportateur
    étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[foreign importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, including a
    government entity, in a foreign country that imports into that
    country hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is
    exported from, or conveyed in transit through, Canada. ([importateur
    étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[interim disposal operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interim disposal operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a disposal
    operation set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 that bears one
    of the disposal codes D13 to D15 set out in column 1 of that
    Part. ([opération préalable d'élimination]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[interim recycling operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interim recycling operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    recycling operation set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1 that
    bears one of the recycling codes R12, R13 and RC3 set out in column
    1 of that Part. ([opération préalable de recyclage]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[line entry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[line entry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a notification, an entry that
      appears on a separate numbered line in the notification with
      respect to a type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material that is to be transported under the permit and that
      includes that line number and the information set out in
      subparagraphs 1(j)(i) to (iii), 2(j)(i) to (iii), 3(i)(i) to
      (iii), 4(i)(i) to (iii), 5(j)(i) to (iii) or 6(j)(i) to (iii) of
      Schedule 3, as the case may be;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a movement document referred to in
      Part 1, an entry that appears on a separate numbered line in the
      movement document with respect to a type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be transported under a
      permit and that includes that line number, the reference number of
      the notification for the permit and the line entry for that type
      of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is set
      out in the notification, with the exception of the information set
      out in clauses 1(j)(iii)(A), (F) and (L), 2(j)(iii)(A), (F) and
      (L), 3(i)(iii)(A), (F) and (L), 4(i)(iii)(A), (F) and (L),
      5(j)(iii)(A), (E) and (K) or 6(j)(iii)(A), (E) and (K) of Schedule
      3, as the case may be; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a movement document referred to in
      Part 2, an entry that appears on a separate numbered line in the
      movement document with respect to a type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be shipped from a
      consignor to a site that is owned, operated or otherwise
      controlled by a consignee and that includes that line number and
      the information set out in paragraph 1(g) of Schedule 4. ([ligne
      de renseignements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[movement document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[movement document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to a
    shipment of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material, a
    document that is referred to

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in sections 15 and 16, if the shipment is
      imported to Canada;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in sections 27 and 28, if the shipment is
      exported from Canada;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in sections 39 and 40, if the shipment is
      exported from Canada and is imported to Canada after it is
      conveyed in transit through a foreign country;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in sections 49 and 50, if the shipment is
      conveyed in transit through Canada;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in sections 60 and 61, if the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is returned to Canada;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in sections 71 and 72, if the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is returned to a foreign country of
      origin; and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in section 80, if the shipment is moved within
      Canada. ([document de mouvement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[municipality]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[municipality]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an incorporated city,
    town, village, metropolitan authority, township, district, county or
    rural municipality in Canada. ([municipalité]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[notification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[notification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a notification referred
    to in paragraph 185(1)(a) of the
    Act. ([notification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL of the Organization for Economic
    Co-operation and Development, entitled Decision of the Council
    Concerning the Revision of Decision C(92)39/FINAL on the Control of
    Transboundary Movements of Wastes Destined for Recovery Operations,
    dated February 25, 2002. ([décision C(2001)107/FINAL de
    l'OCDE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[PCB]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[PCB]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means polychlorinated
    biphenyl. ([BPC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permit referred to in
    paragraph 185(1)(b) of the Act. ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[persistent organic pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[persistent organic pollutant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    substance set out in column 3 of Schedule 5 that is contained in
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material, if the
    concentration is equal to or greater than the concentration set out
    in column 4 of that Schedule. ([substance polluante organique
    persistante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[receiving facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[receiving facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the first
    authorized facility that receives or is to receive hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material for disposal or recycling under a
    permit or permits, whether the disposal or recycling operation that
    is to be performed at that facility is interim or
    final. ([installation de réception]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recyclable material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recyclable material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means anything that is
    to be recycled. ([matière recyclable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cargo, in one or more
    containers or in bulk, that is transported as a unit without being
    separated at any time during the movement and that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a return to Canada or to a foreign
      country of origin, is shipped to the facility from which it was
      originally shipped or to the facility identified in the permit for
      the return;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a movement within Canada, is
      shipped by a consignor to a site that is located in another
      province and that is owned, operated or otherwise controlled by a
      consignee; and 

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, is shipped by one person from
      one facility in the country of origin to a receiving facility in
      the country of destination. ([envoi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sign]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sign]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes to sign by means of an
    electronic signature that is generated using a technology or process
    that is approved by the Minister for the purposes of submitting
    information to the Minister online. ([signature]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[unique identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[unique identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a number
    assigned by a province or a country to identify a person, entity or
    facility. ([numéro d'identification unique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means anything that is to be disposed
    of. ([déchet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[hazardous
waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of Division 8 of Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act and these
  Regulations, [[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means anything
  that is to be disposed of using one of the operations set out in
  column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 and that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is set out in column 2 of Schedule 6;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meets the criteria for inclusion in one of Classes
    2 to 6, 8 and 9 as set out in Part 2 of the [Transportation of
    Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} contains a substance set out in column 3 of
    Schedule 7 that is in a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration set out in column 4 of that Schedule;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} produces a leachate that contains an
    environmentally hazardous constituent set out in column 3 of
    Schedule 2 that is in a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration set out in column 4 of that Schedule; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is set out in column 3 of Schedule 8, is either
    pure or the only active ingredient and is unused.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Hazardous waste referred to in subsection (1) does
  not include anything

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is to be transported in a quantity of less
    than 5 kg or 5 L per shipment, unless it contains mercury or meets
    the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the
    [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that remains in a container that is to be
    transported after the contents of that container have been removed
    to the maximum extent feasible and before the container is either
    refilled or cleaned of its residual contents;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is mingled with non-hazardous waste or
    non-hazardous recyclable material and collected as part of the
    regular collection of non-hazardous waste and non-hazardous
    recyclable material by or on behalf of a municipality, unless it is
    separated from that waste or recyclable material during or after the
    collection;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is personal or household waste of the
    individual who transports it; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is generated from the normal operations of a
    ship and whose discharge is authorized under the Canada Shipping
    Act, 2001.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waste considered hazardous for export

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Anything
  that is to be disposed of using one of the operations set out in
  column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1, even if it is not [[hazardous
  waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of subsection 2(1),
  is considered to be hazardous waste for the purposes of Division 8 of
  Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act and Divisions 1 to 4, 7 and 8 of Part 1
  of these Regulations if it is to be exported to a country of
  destination or conveyed in transit through Canada or a foreign country
  and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is defined as, or considered to be, hazardous
    under the legislation of the country of destination or country of
    transit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its import is prohibited under the legislation of
    the country of destination; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is one of the hazardous wastes referred to in
    Article 1, subparagraph 1(a) of the Convention or of the other
    wastes referred to in Article 1, paragraph 2 of the Convention, with
    the exception of wastes referred to in Article 1, paragraphs 3 and 4
    of the Convention --- as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
    time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
    Canada --- and the country of destination is a party to the
    Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waste considered hazardous for return
  to foreign country of origin

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is to be disposed of using one of the
  operations set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1, even if it is
  not [[hazardous waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of
  subsection 2(1), is considered to be hazardous waste for the purposes
  of Division 8 of Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act and Division 6 of Part
  1 of these Regulations if it is to be conveyed in transit through a
  foreign country and it is defined as, or considered to be, hazardous
  under the legislation of that country.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[hazardous recyclable
material]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of Division 8 of Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act and these
  Regulations, [[hazardous recyclable material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means anything that is to be recycled using one of the operations set
  out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1 and that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is set out in column 2 of Schedule 6;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meets the criteria for inclusion in one of Classes
    2 to 6, 8 and 9 as set out in Part 2 of the [Transportation of
    Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} contains a substance set out in column 3 of
    Schedule 7 that is in a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration set out in column 4 of that Schedule;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} produces a leachate that contains an
    environmentally hazardous constituent set out in column 3 of
    Schedule 2 that is in a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration set out in column 4 of that Schedule; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is set out in column 3 of Schedule 8, is either
    pure or the only active ingredient and is unused.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions --- import, export and
  transit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the provisions of Division 8 of
  Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act that relate to the import, export and
  conveyance in transit of hazardous recyclable material and for the
  purposes of Part 1 of these Regulations, hazardous recyclable material
  referred to in subsection (1) does not include anything

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is to be transported in a quantity of less
    than 5 kg or 5 L per shipment, unless it contains mercury or meets
    the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the
    [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that remains in a container that is to be
    transported after the contents of that container have been removed
    to the maximum extent feasible and before the container is either
    refilled or cleaned of its residual contents;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is mingled with non-hazardous waste or
    non-hazardous recyclable material and collected as part of the
    regular collection of non-hazardous waste and non-hazardous
    recyclable material by or on behalf of a municipality, unless it is
    separated from that waste or recyclable material during or after the
    collection;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is personal or household recyclable material
    of the individual who transports it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is generated from the normal operations of a
    ship and whose discharge is authorized under the [Canada Shipping
    Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that is to be imported from a country of origin, or
    exported to a country of destination, that is subject to OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, and, if applicable, that is to be
    conveyed in transit only through a country that is subject to that
    Decision, if it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is to be imported or exported in a quantity of
      25 kg or 25 L or less per shipment for the purpose of conducting
      analysis or research with respect to the recycling of that
      recyclable material,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} does not contain an infectious substance that
      meets the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2
      of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is accompanied by a document that includes the
      name and address of the importer or exporter and the words "test
      samples" or "[échantillons d'épreuve]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} that is to be imported from a country of origin, or
    exported to a country of destination, that is subject to OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, and, if applicable, that is to be
    conveyed in transit only through a country that is a party to that
    Decision, if it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is set out in Schedule 9,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is to be recycled by means of a recycling
      operation set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1 at an
      authorized facility in the country of destination, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is accompanied by one or more documents that
      demonstrate that the requirements of this paragraph are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions --- movement within Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the provisions of Division 8 of
  Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act that relate to the movement of hazardous
  recyclable material within Canada and for the purposes of Part 2 of
  these Regulations, hazardous recyclable material referred to in
  subsection (1) does not include anything

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is to be transported in a quantity of less
    than 5 kg or 5 L per shipment, unless it contains mercury or meets
    the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the
    [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that remains in a container that is to be
    transported after the contents of that container have been removed
    to the maximum extent feasible and before the container is either
    refilled or cleaned of its residual contents;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is mingled with non-hazardous waste or
    non-hazardous recyclable material and collected as part of the
    regular collection of non-hazardous waste and non-hazardous
    recyclable material by or on behalf of a municipality, unless it is
    separated from that waste or recyclable material during or after the
    collection;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is personal or household recyclable material
    of the individual who transports it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is generated from the normal operations of a
    ship and whose discharge is authorized under the [Canada Shipping
    Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that satisfies the following conditions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is to be transported in a quantity of 25 kg or
      25 L or less per shipment for the purpose of conducting analysis
      or research with respect to the recycling of that recyclable
      material,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it does not contain an infectious substance that
      meets the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2
      of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it is accompanied by a document that includes
      the name and address of the consignor or consignee and the words
      "test samples" or "[échantillons d'épreuve]{.otherLang
      lang="fr"}";

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} that satisfies the following conditions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is set out in Schedule 9, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is to be recycled by means of a recycling
      operation set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} that satisfies the following conditions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} mercury is a component of it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the mercury it contains is to be transported in
      a quantity of 50 mL or less per shipment,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it has reached the end of its useful life, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} it is to be recycled by means of a recycling
      operation set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1; or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that satisfies the following conditions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is a non-rechargeable or rechargeable cell or
      battery that does not meet the criteria in paragraph (1)(b), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is to be recycled by means of a recycling
      operation set out in Column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recyclable material considered hazardous
for export

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Anything
  that is to be recycled using one of the operations set out in column 2
  of Part 2 of Schedule 1, even if it is not [[hazardous recyclable
  material]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of subsection
  4(1), is considered to be hazardous recyclable material for the
  purposes of Division 8 of Part 7 and Part 10 of the Act and Divisions
  1 to 5, 7 and 8 of Part 1 of these Regulations if it is to be exported
  to a country of destination or conveyed in transit through Canada or a
  foreign country and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is defined as, or considered to be, hazardous
    under the legislation of the country of destination or country of
    transit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its import is prohibited under the legislation of
    the country of destination; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is one of the hazardous wastes referred to in
    Article 1, subparagraph 1(a) of the Convention or of the other
    wastes referred to in Article 1, paragraph 2 of the Convention, with
    the exception of wastes referred to in Article 1, paragraphs 3 and 4
    of the Convention --- as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
    time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
    Canada --- and the country of destination is a party to the
    Convention.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recyclable material considered
  hazardous for return to foreign country of origin

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is to be recycled using one of the
  operations set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1, even if it is
  not [[hazardous recyclable material]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the
  meaning of subsection 4(1), is considered to be hazardous recyclable
  material for the purposes of Division 8 of Part 7 and Part 10 of the
  Act and Division 6 of Part 1 of these Regulations if it is to be
  conveyed in transit through a foreign country and it is defined as, or
  considered to be, hazardous under the legislation of that country.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Import, Export and Transit]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1286477 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Import]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1286478 .Subheading}

#### [Non-application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286479 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- Department of
National Defence

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Paragraphs 8(4)(b) to (e)
and sections 14 to 18 do not apply to the import of hazardous waste or
hazardous recyclable material by the Department of National Defence if
that hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is generated by
the Department in the course of an operation conducted by it outside
Canada and is transported from the site of the operation to a [[defence
establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of
the [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5), in or on a means of transport

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is owned and operated by the Department or that
  is operated on behalf of the Department by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the Department,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a member of the Canadian Forces, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other person, if the means of transport is
    accompanied at all times by, and is under the direct responsibility
    of, an employee of the Department or a member of the Canadian
    Forces; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is owned and operated by the military
  establishment of a member country of the North Atlantic Treaty
  Organization, or by the military establishment of another country
  under an agreement with the Department, or that is operated on behalf
  of such an establishment by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} military or civilian personnel of that
    establishment, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other person, if the means of transport is
    accompanied at all times by, and is under the direct responsibility
    of, military or civilian personnel of that establishment.

#### [Import Permit]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286489 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** A person may apply for a
permit to import hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are an individual who is a resident of Canada or
  a corporation that has its head office, or operates a branch office,
  in Canada, and they

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are the owner or operator of the receiving
    facility, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} buy and sell --- or are to buy and sell ---
    hazardous recyclable material for recycling and are to import such
    recyclable material for recycling in Canada; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they are a municipality or Her Majesty in right of
  Canada or a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of application

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be made to the Minister by means of a
  notification that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is
  dated and signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must relate to either hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material but not to both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more shipments that contain
    the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must involve only

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one foreign exporter,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one facility in the country of origin from which
      the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      shipped, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one receiving facility in Canada to which the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      delivered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 1(j)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 1(j)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 1 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant satisfies the conditions in section
    7;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and all authorized carriers that are
    to transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
    Canada hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all aspects of the proposed import of the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and of a possible return
    under Division 6 of this Part in the circumstances set out in
    clauses 14(1)(u)(iii)(B) and (v)(iii)(B), other than transportation
    by an authorized carrier, are governed by one or more contracts to
    which the applicant, the foreign exporter and the facility in the
    country of origin from which the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material is to be shipped are parties;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all aspects of the proposed disposal of the
    hazardous waste or recycling of the hazardous recyclable material
    are governed by one or more contracts to which the receiving
    facility and the applicant are parties;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of
    hazardous waste or recycle hazardous recyclable material under the
    permit or permits by means of an interim disposal operation or
    interim recycling operation, all aspects of the proposed disposal of
    the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or the
    proposed recycling of the hazardous recyclable material by means of
    a final recycling operation, are governed by one or more contracts
    to which the receiving facility and the authorized facility that is
    to perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation
    are parties;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing and manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the
    shipment that contains the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is to be shipped, in the case of a single shipment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the first
    shipment is to be shipped, in the case of multiple shipments.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse to
issue a permit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authorization referred to in subparagraph
  185(1)(b)(i) of the Act is not obtained, unless the circumstances in
  subsection 185(4) of the Act apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that,
  if the permit were issued, the movement of hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or
  recycling of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit would not
  comply with the Act, these Regulations or conditions to an
  authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of destination or a country
  of transit; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  false or misleading information was provided in the notification under
  section 8.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 36 months after the day on which it is issued, in the
  case of hazardous recyclable material

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is to be imported from a country of origin
    that is subject to OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL and, if
    applicable, conveyed in transit only through a country that is
    subject to that Decision, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that is to be recycled by means of a final
    recycling operation at a receiving facility that is a pre-consented
    recovery facility referred to in Case 2 in Chapter II. D (2) of OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, as amended from time to time, to the
    extent that the amendments are binding on Canada; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 12 months after the day on which it is issued, in any
  other case.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit if an authority referred to in
  subparagraph 185(1)(b)(i) of the Act withdraws an authorization
  referred to in that subparagraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder no longer satisfies the
    conditions in section 7;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit does not comply
    --- or will not comply --- with the Act, these Regulations or the
    conditions of an authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of
    destination or a country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 8;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, cannot be in accordance with the
    permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension or if the permit holder requests the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286569 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Import]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286570 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed
  conditions for the import of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material under one or more permits that are held by a permit holder
  are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the movement, and the disposal or recycling, of the
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be in
    accordance with the permit or permits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the import must not be prohibited under the laws of
    Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin must be a party to the
    Convention or the Canada-USA Agreement or must be subject to OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL and the export of the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material must not be prohibited by that
    country;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the conveyance in transit of the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material through a country must not be
    prohibited by that country;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} biomedical waste set out in item 1 of Schedule 6 or
    anything that meets the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set
    out in Part 2 of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) must be disposed of by
    means of a final disposal operation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if anything contains PCBs in a concentration
    greater than 50 mg per kg, the PCBs in that thing or in any residue
    from its decontamination must be destroyed or irreversibly
    transformed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder and all authorized carriers that
    transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
    Canada must hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7
    of this Part;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} all of the contracts referred to in paragraphs
    8(4)(c) to (e) must be in effect;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraph 8(4)(c)
    must

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describe each type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be imported under the
      contract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out the quantity of each type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identify the operation set out in column 2 of
      Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that is to be performed on each type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} state that the hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material is to be imported in accordance with the
      permit, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} require the foreign exporter and the facility to
      take all feasible measures to assist the permit holder to satisfy
      the conditions under paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraphs 8(4)(d)
    and (e) must

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describe each type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be imported under the
      contract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out the quantity of each type of hazardous
      waste that is to be disposed of, or hazardous recyclable material
      that is to be recycled, at the authorized facility,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identify the operation set out in column 2 of
      Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that is to be performed on each type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material at the authorized
      facility,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} state that the hazardous waste is to be disposed
      of, or the hazardous recyclable material is to be recycled, in
      accordance with the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} require the authorized facility to notify the
      permit holder in writing, within 30 days after the day on which
      the authorized facility completes an interim or final disposal
      operation for a type of hazardous waste, or an interim or final
      recycling operation for a type of hazardous recyclable material,
      that is recorded as a line entry in the movement document,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} of the date on which the operation was
        completed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} of the reference number of the movement
        document, and the line number of the line entry in the movement
        document, that applies to that type of hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} that the operation was completed in a manner
        that protects the environment and human health against the
        adverse effects that may result from the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} require the authorized facility to take all
      feasible measures to assist the permit holder to satisfy the
      conditions under paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraph 8(4)(e)
    must require the receiving facility to provide the authorized
    facility that is to dispose of a type of hazardous waste or recycle
    a type of hazardous recyclable material that was contained in the
    shipment by means of a final disposal operation or final recycling
    operation with the reference number of the movement document and the
    line number of the line entry in the movement document that applies
    to that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} each shipment must be recorded in a separate
    movement document in accordance with sections 15 and 16;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
    section 16 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
    Minister and the authorities of the province where the receiving
    facility is located;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
    carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
    movement document completed in accordance with section 16, when they
    are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of the
    shipment complies with this Division;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
    movement document completed in accordance with section 16, must be
    provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
    be reported at the customs office under section 12 of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of a type
    of hazardous waste or recycle a type of hazardous recyclable
    material under the permit or permits by means of an interim disposal
    operation or interim recycling operation, it must complete the
    disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the recycling of all of
    the hazardous recyclable material, of that type that is contained in
    a shipment, either within a period of 180 days after the day on
    which the shipment is delivered to it or within a shorter period
    required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which it is
    located;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of a type
    of hazardous waste or recycle a type of hazardous recyclable
    material under the permit or permits by means of a final disposal
    operation or final recycling operation, it must complete the
    disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the recycling of all of
    the hazardous recyclable material, of that type that is contained in
    a shipment, either within a period of one year after the day on
    which the shipment is delivered to it or within a shorter period
    required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which it is
    located;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} if an authorized facility other than a receiving
    facility is to dispose of a type of hazardous waste or recycle a
    type of hazardous recyclable material under the permit or permits by
    means of a final disposal operation or final recycling operation, it
    must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the
    recycling of all of the hazardous recyclable material, of that type
    that was contained in a shipment delivered to the receiving facility
    and that was sent to the authorized facility by the receiving
    facility, either within a period of 18 months after the day on which
    the shipment was delivered to the receiving facility or within a
    shorter period required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in
    which the authorized facility is located;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} the Minister must be provided with confirmation of
    the disposal of the hazardous waste by means of an operation set out
    in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1, or of the recycling of the
    hazardous recyclable material by means of an operation set out in
    column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1, in accordance with section 17;

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 18 must be
    kept in accordance with that section;

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} if the permit holder is notified that the receiving
    facility does not accept hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material that is contained in a shipment that is shipped in
    accordance with the Act and these Regulations, or that it cannot, or
    refuses to, dispose of that hazardous waste or recycle that
    hazardous recyclable material in accordance with the permit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder must immediately notify the
      Minister and the foreign exporter of the situation and the reason
      for it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if necessary, the permit holder must store the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in a facility
      that is authorized for that purpose by the authorities of the
      jurisdiction in which the facility is located,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} within 90 days after the day on which the
      Minister is notified or within any other period of time that is
      agreed to by the Minister and the competent authority of the
      country of origin, the permit holder must either

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} make all necessary arrangements to dispose of
        the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or
        to recycle the hazardous recyclable material by means of a final
        recycling operation, at another authorized facility in Canada
        and provide the Minister with the name and address of, and the
        name of a contact person for, that authorized facility, the
        reference number of the movement document and, for each type of
        hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material, the line
        number of the applicable line entry in the movement document,
        the quantity in kilograms or litres and the applicable disposal
        or recycling code set out in column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of
        Schedule 1, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with Division 6 of this Part,
        return the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to
        the country of origin under an export permit for the return,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material must not be transported to an authorized facility
      referred to in clause (iii)(A) unless the permit holder has
      received confirmation from the Minister that the authorities of
      the jurisdiction in which the authorized facility is located have
      approved its disposal or recycling at that authorized facility,
      and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is transported to an authorized facility referred to in
      clause (iii)(A) for disposal or recycling, the authorized facility
      must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste by means
      of a final disposal operation, or the recycling of all of the
      hazardous recyclable material by means of a final recycling
      operation, within one year after the expiry of the period referred
      to in subparagraph (iii); and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if a shipment is imported in accordance with the
    Act and these Regulations but the Minister notifies the permit
    holder that the Minister does not accept hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material that is contained in the shipment or
    that hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is
    contained in the shipment cannot be moved, disposed of or recycled
    in accordance with the permit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder must immediately notify the
      foreign exporter of the situation and the reason for it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if necessary, the permit holder must store the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in a facility
      that is identified for that purpose by the Minister,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} within 90 days after the day on which the
      Minister notifies the permit holder or within any other period of
      time that is agreed to by the Minister and the competent authority
      of the country of origin, the permit holder must either

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} make all necessary arrangements to dispose of
        the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or
        to recycle the hazardous recyclable material by means of a final
        recycling operation, at an authorized facility in Canada and
        provide the Minister with the name and address of, and the name
        of a contact person for, that authorized facility, the reference
        number of the movement document and, for each type of hazardous
        waste or hazardous recyclable material, the line number of the
        applicable line entry in the movement document, the quantity in
        kilograms or litres and the applicable disposal or recycling
        code set out in column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with Division 6 of this Part,
        return the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to
        the country of origin under an export permit for the return,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material must not be transported to an authorized facility
      referred to in clause (iii)(A) unless the permit holder has
      received confirmation from the Minister that the authorities of
      the jurisdiction in which the authorized facility is located have
      approved its disposal or recycling at that authorized facility,
      and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is transported to an authorized facility referred to in
      clause (iii)(A) for disposal or recycling, the authorized facility
      must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste by means
      of a final disposal operation, or the recycling of all of the
      hazardous recyclable material by means of a final recycling
      operation, within one year after the expiry of the period referred
      to in subparagraph (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to receiving facility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(p) to (r),
  delivery of a shipment to the receiving facility occurs when the
  shipment arrives at the receiving facility, regardless of whether or
  when hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is
  contained in the shipment is accepted by the receiving facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Necessary arrangements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The necessary arrangements referred to in clauses
  (1)(u)(iii)(A) and (v)(iii)(A) must include a requirement that the
  authorized facility notify the permit holder in writing, within 30
  days after the day on which the authorized facility completes a final
  disposal operation for a type of hazardous waste, or a final recycling
  operation for a type of hazardous recyclable material, that is
  recorded as a line entry in the movement document,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of the date on which the operation was completed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of the reference number of the movement document,
    and the line number of the line entry in the movement document, that
    applies to that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the operation was completed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(v)(iii), the
  Minister notifies the permit holder when the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice is available through a
    generally accessible electronic source and identifies the location
    of the notice; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice to the permit holder's last known
    address or email address.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286635 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped from a facility under one or more permits, Part A
  of the movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment and that was
    recorded as a line entry in the notification under subsection 8(3)
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each line entry must include the reference number
    of the notification, the line entry number and the information that
    was included in the line entry in the notification under subsection
    8(4) --- with the exception of the information referred to in
    clauses 1(j)(iii)(A), (F) and (L) of Schedule 3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in subitem 1(1)
    of Schedule 10 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to Minister and provincial
  authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped from the facility, Part A of the movement document
  must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the Minister, to
  the Minister and to the authorities of the province where the
  receiving facility is located, if those authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  must complete Part B of the movement document --- of which Part A was
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after the shipment is delivered to the
  receiving facility, Part C of the movement document --- of which Part
  A was completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- must be
  completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 3(1) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C to Minister and
  provincial authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the receiving facility, Part B of the
  movement document completed by each authorized carrier and Part C of
  the movement document must be provided, in the form and manner
  specified by the Minister, to the Minister and to the authorities of
  the province where the receiving facility is located, if those
  authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to Part C

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Part C of the movement document may be amended only
  with respect to the quantities specified in it. The amendments must be
  made in the form and manner specified by the Minister and before the
  confirmation under subsection 17(1) is to be filed with the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to receiving facility

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) and (5), delivery
  of a shipment to the receiving facility occurs when the shipment
  arrives at the receiving facility, regardless of whether or when
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is contained in
  the shipment is accepted by the receiving facility.

##### [Confirmation of Disposal or Recycling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286659 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within
  30 days after the day on which the disposal by means of an operation
  set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 of a type of hazardous
  waste, or the recycling by means of an operation set out in column 2
  of Part 2 of Schedule 1 of a type of hazardous recyclable material,
  that is recorded as a line entry in a movement document is completed,
  the Minister must be provided with a confirmation, based on the
  written notice received by the permit holder from the authorized
  facility that performed the operation, that the operation was
  completed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the permit or, if applicable,
    with the arrangements referred to in clause 14(1)(u)(iii)(A) or
    (v)(iii)(A);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within the period set out in one of paragraphs
    14(1)(p) to (r) or subparagraph 14(1)(u)(v) or (v)(v), as the case
    may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a manner that protects the environment and human
    health against the adverse effects that may result from the
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner of confirmation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The confirmation must be provided in the form and
  manner specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information in confirmation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The confirmation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must identify the reference number of the movement
    document, the line number of the line entry in the movement document
    and the disposal or recycling code set out in column 1 of either
    Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that applies to the type of hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and the date on which the
    operation was completed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must be dated and signed by the individual who
    provides it and must include that individual's name and telephone
    number.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286672 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  permit holder must keep the following documents that relate to
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is transported
  under one or more permits at their principal place of business in
  Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on which any of
  the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    their compliance with this Division;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every notice that is referred to in subparagraph
    14(1)(j)(v) or subsection 14(3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all contracts that relate to the movement, and the
    disposal or recycling, of the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that they hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported under one or more permits at their principal place of
  business in Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on
  which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the authorized carrier transported the shipment
    in Canada, all documents that are necessary to demonstrate that they
    hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Export]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1286684 .Subheading}

#### [Export Permit]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286685 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** A person may apply for a
permit to export hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are an individual who is a resident of Canada or
  a corporation that has its head office, or operates a branch office,
  in Canada, and they

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are the owner or operator of the facility from
    which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
    shipped, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} buy and sell --- or are to buy and sell ---
    hazardous recyclable material for recycling and are to export such
    recyclable material for recycling in a country of destination that
    is subject to OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they are a municipality or Her Majesty in right of
  Canada or a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of application

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be made to the Minister by means of a
  notification that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is
  dated and signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must relate to either hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material but not to both;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more shipments that contain
    the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must involve only

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one foreign importer,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one facility in Canada from which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one receiving facility in the country of
      destination to which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be delivered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 2(j)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 2(j)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 2 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant satisfies the conditions set out in
    section 19;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and all authorized carriers that are
    to transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
    Canada hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all aspects of the proposed export of the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and of a possible return
    under Division 5 of this Part in the circumstances set out in
    clauses 26(1)(v)(iii)(B) and (w)(iii)(B), other than transportation
    by an authorized carrier, are governed by one or more contracts to
    which the applicant, the foreign importer and the facility in Canada
    from which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is
    to be shipped are parties;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all aspects of the proposed disposal of the
    hazardous waste or recycling of the hazardous recyclable material
    are governed by one or more contracts to which the receiving
    facility and the applicant are parties;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of
    hazardous waste or recycle hazardous recyclable material under the
    permit or permits by means of an interim disposal operation or
    interim recycling operation, all aspects of the proposed disposal of
    the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or the
    proposed recycling of the hazardous recyclable material by means of
    a final recycling operation, are governed by one or more contracts
    to which the receiving facility and the authorized facility that is
    to perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation
    are parties;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing and manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the
    shipment that contains the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is to be shipped, in the case of a single shipment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the first
    shipment is to be shipped, in the case of multiple shipments.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse
to issue a permit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authorization referred to in subparagraph
  185(1)(b)(i) of the Act is not obtained, unless the circumstances in
  subsection 185(4) of the Act apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that,
  if the permit were issued, the movement of hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or
  recycling of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit would not
  comply with the Act, these Regulations or conditions to an
  authorization imposed by the country of destination or a country of
  transit; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  false or misleading information was provided in the notification under
  section 20.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 36 months after the day on which it is issued, in the
  case of hazardous recyclable material

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is to be exported to a country of destination
    that is subject to OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL and, if
    applicable, conveyed in transit only through a country that is
    subject to that Decision, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that is to be recycled by means of a final
    recycling operation at a receiving facility that is a pre-consented
    recovery facility referred to in Case 2 in Chapter II. D (2) of OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, as amended from time to time, to the
    extent that the amendments are binding on Canada; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 12 months after the day on which it is issued, in any
  other case.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit if an authority referred to in
