  subparagraph 185(1)(b)(i) of the Act withdraws an authorization
  referred to in that subparagraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder no longer satisfies the
    conditions set out in section 19;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit does not comply
    --- or will not comply --- with the Act, these Regulations or the
    conditions of an authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of
    destination or a country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 20;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, cannot be in accordance with the
    permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension or if the permit holder requests the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286765 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Export]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286766 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed
  conditions for the export of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material under one or more permits that are held by a permit holder
  are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the movement, and the disposal or recycling, of the
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be in
    accordance with the permit or permits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the export must not be prohibited under the laws of
    Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the country of destination must be a party to the
    Convention or the Canada-USA Agreement or must be subject to OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL and the import of the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material must not be prohibited by that
    country;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the conveyance in transit of the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material through a country must not be
    prohibited by that country;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material must not be disposed of or recycled south of 60° south
    latitude;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} biomedical waste set out in item 1 of Schedule 6 or
    anything that meets the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set
    out in Part 2 of the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) must be disposed of by
    means of a final disposal operation;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if anything contains PCBs in a concentration
    greater than 50 mg per kg, the PCBs in that thing or in any residue
    from its decontamination must be destroyed or irreversibly
    transformed;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder and all authorized carriers that
    transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
    Canada must hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7
    of this Part;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all of the contracts referred to in paragraphs
    20(4)(c) to (e) must be in effect;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraph 20(4)(c)
    must

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describe each type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be exported under the
      contract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out the quantity of each type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identify the operation set out in column 2 of
      Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that is to be performed on each type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} state that the hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material is to be exported in accordance with the
      permit, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} require the foreign importer and the facility to
      take all feasible measures to assist the permit holder to satisfy
      the conditions under paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraphs 20(4)(d)
    and (e) must

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describe each type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be exported under the
      contract,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} set out the quantity of each type of hazardous
      waste that is to be disposed of, or hazardous recyclable material
      that is to be recycled, at the authorized facility,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} identify the operation set out in column 2 of
      Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that is to be performed on each type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material at the authorized
      facility,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} state that the hazardous waste is to be disposed
      of, or the hazardous recyclable material is to be recycled, in
      accordance with the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} require the authorized facility to notify the
      permit holder in writing, within 30 days after the day on which
      the authorized facility completes an interim or final disposal
      operation for a type of hazardous waste, or an interim or final
      recycling operation for a type of hazardous recyclable material,
      that is recorded as a line entry in the movement document,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} of the date on which the operation was
        completed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} of the reference number of the movement
        document, and the line number of the line entry in the movement
        document, that applies to that type of hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} that the operation was completed in a manner
        that protects the environment and human health against the
        adverse effects that may result from the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} require the authorized facility to take all
      feasible measures to assist the permit holder to satisfy the
      conditions under paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} every contract referred to in paragraph 20(4)(e)
    must require the receiving facility to provide the authorized
    facility that is to dispose of a type of hazardous waste or recycle
    a type of hazardous recyclable material that was contained in the
    shipment by means of a final disposal operation or final recycling
    operation with the reference number of the movement document and the
    line number of the line entry in the movement document that applies
    to that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} each shipment must be recorded in a separate
    movement document in accordance with sections 27 and 28;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
    section 28 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
    Minister and the authorities of the province where the facility from
    which the shipment was shipped is located;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
    carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
    movement document completed in accordance with section 28, when they
    are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of the
    shipment complies with this Division;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
    movement document completed in accordance with section 28, must be
    provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
    be reported at the customs office under section 95 of the [Customs
    Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of a type
    of hazardous waste or recycle a type of hazardous recyclable
    material under the permit or permits by means of an interim disposal
    operation or interim recycling operation, it must complete the
    disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the recycling of all of
    the hazardous recyclable material, of that type that is contained in
    a shipment, either within a period of 180 days after the day on
    which the shipment is delivered to it or within a shorter period
    required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which it is
    located;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} if the receiving facility is to dispose of a type
    of hazardous waste or recycle a type of hazardous recyclable
    material under the permit or permits by means of a final disposal
    operation or final recycling operation, it must complete the
    disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the recycling of all of
    the hazardous recyclable material, of that type that is contained in
    a shipment, either within a period of one year after the day on
    which the shipment is delivered to it or within a shorter period
    required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which it is
    located;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} if an authorized facility other than a receiving
    facility is to dispose of a type of hazardous waste or recycle a
    type of hazardous recyclable material under the permit or permits by
    means of a final disposal operation or final recycling operation, it
    must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste, or the
    recycling of all of the hazardous recyclable material, of that type
    that was contained in a shipment delivered to the receiving facility
    and that was sent to the authorized facility by the receiving
    facility, either within a period of 18 months after the day on which
    the shipment was delivered to the receiving facility or within a
    shorter period required by the authorities of the jurisdiction in
    which the authorized facility is located;

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} the Minister must be provided with confirmation of
    the disposal of the hazardous waste by means of an operation set out
    in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1, or of the recycling of the
    hazardous recyclable material by means of an operation set out in
    column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 1, in accordance with section 29;

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 30 must be
    kept in accordance with that section;

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the permit holder is notified that the receiving
    facility does not accept hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material that is contained in a shipment that is shipped in
    accordance with the Act and these Regulations, or that it cannot, or
    refuses to, dispose of that hazardous waste or recycle that
    hazardous recyclable material in accordance with the permit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder must immediately notify the
      Minister and the foreign importer of the situation and the reason
      for it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder must store the hazardous waste
      or hazardous recyclable material in a facility that is authorized
      for that purpose by the authorities of the jurisdiction in which
      the facility is located,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} within 90 days after the day on which the
      Minister is notified or within any other period of time that is
      agreed to by the Minister and the competent authority of the
      country of destination, the permit holder must either

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} make all necessary arrangements to dispose of
        the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or
        to recycle the hazardous recyclable material by means of a final
        recycling operation, at another authorized facility in the
        country of destination and provide the Minister with the name
        and address of, and the name of a contact person for, that
        authorized facility, the reference number of the movement
        document and, for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material, the line number of the applicable line
        entry in the movement document, the quantity in kilograms or
        litres and the applicable disposal or recycling code set out in
        column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with Division 5 of this Part,
        return the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to
        Canada under an import permit for the return,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material must not be transported to an authorized facility
      referred to in clause (iii)(A) unless the permit holder has
      received confirmation from the Minister that the competent
      authority of the country of destination has approved its disposal
      or recycling at that authorized facility, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is transported to an authorized facility referred to in
      clause (iii)(A) for disposal or recycling, the authorized facility
      must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste by means
      of a final disposal operation, or the recycling of all of the
      hazardous recyclable material by means of a final recycling
      operation, within one year after the expiry of the period referred
      to in subparagraph (iii); and

  - [(w)]{.lawlabel} if a shipment is exported in accordance with the
    Act and these Regulations but the competent authority of either the
    country of destination or a country of transit notifies the permit
    holder that it does not accept hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment or that
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is contained
    in the shipment cannot be moved, disposed of or recycled, in
    accordance with the permit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder must immediately notify the
      Minister and the foreign importer of the situation and the reason
      for it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if necessary, the permit holder must store the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in a facility
      that is identified for that purpose by that competent authority,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} within 90 days after the day on which the
      Minister is notified or within any other period of time that is
      agreed to by the Minister and that competent authority, the permit
      holder must either

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} make all necessary arrangements to dispose of
        the hazardous waste by means of a final disposal operation, or
        to recycle the hazardous recyclable material by means of a final
        recycling operation, at an authorized facility in the country
        that is represented by the competent authority and provide the
        Minister with the name and address of, and the name of a contact
        person for, that authorized facility, the reference number of
        the movement document and, for each type of hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material, the line number of the applicable
        line entry in the movement document, the quantity in kilograms
        or litres and the applicable disposal or recycling code set out
        in column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with Division 5 of this Part,
        return the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to
        Canada under an import permit for the return,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material must not be transported to an authorized facility
      referred to in clause (iii)(A) unless the permit holder has
      received confirmation from the Minister that the competent
      authority of the country in which the authorized facility is
      located has approved its disposal or recycling at that authorized
      facility, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is transported to an authorized facility referred to in
      clause (iii)(A) for disposal or recycling, the authorized facility
      must complete the disposal of all of the hazardous waste by means
      of a final disposal operation, or the recycling of all of the
      hazardous recyclable material by means of a final recycling
      operation, within one year after the expiry of the period referred
      to in subparagraph (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to receiving facility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(q) to (s),
  delivery of a shipment to the receiving facility occurs when the
  shipment arrives at the receiving facility, regardless of whether or
  when hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is
  contained in the shipment is accepted by the receiving facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Necessary arrangements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The necessary arrangements referred to in clauses
  (1)(v)(iii)(A) and (w)(iii)(A) must include a requirement that the
  authorized facility notify the permit holder in writing, within 30
  days after the day on which the authorized facility completes a final
  disposal operation for a type of hazardous waste, or a final recycling
  operation for a type of hazardous recyclable material, that is
  recorded as a line entry in the movement document,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of the date on which the operation was completed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of the reference number of the movement document,
    and the line number of the line entry in the movement document, that
    applies to that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the operation was completed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286828 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped from a facility under one or more permits, Part A
  of the movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment and that was
    recorded as a line entry in the notification under subsection 20(3)
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each line entry must include the reference number
    of the notification, the line entry number and the information that
    was included in the line entry in the notification under subsection
    20(4), with the exception of the information referred to in clauses
    2(j)(iii)(A), (F) and (L) of Schedule 3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in subitem 1(2)
    of Schedule 10 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to Minister and provincial
  authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped from the facility, Part A of the movement document
  must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the Minister, to
  the Minister and to the authorities of the province where the facility
  is located, if those authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  must complete Part B of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after the shipment is delivered to the
  receiving facility, Part C of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- must be completed as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 3(1) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C to Minister and
  provincial authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the receiving facility, Part B of the
  movement document completed by each authorized carrier and Part C of
  the movement document must be provided, in the form and manner
  specified by the Minister, to the Minister and to the authorities of
  the province where the facility from which the shipment was shipped is
  located, if those authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to Part C

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Part C of the movement document may be amended only
  with respect to the quantities specified in it. The amendments must be
  made in the form and manner specified by the Minister and before the
  confirmation under subsection 29(1) is to be filed with the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to receiving facility

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) and (5), delivery
  of a shipment to the receiving facility occurs when the shipment
  arrives at the receiving facility, regardless of whether or when
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is contained in
  the shipment is accepted by the receiving facility.

##### [Confirmation of Disposal or Recycling]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286852 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within
  30 days after the day on which the disposal by means of an operation
  set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 1 of a type of hazardous
  waste, or the recycling by means of an operation set out in column 2
  of Part 2 of Schedule 1 of a type of hazardous recyclable material,
  that is recorded as a line entry in a movement document is completed,
  the Minister must be provided with a confirmation, based on the
  written notice received by the permit holder from the authorized
  facility that performed the operation, that the operation was
  completed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the permit or, if applicable,
    with the arrangements referred to in clause 26(1)(v)(iii)(A) or
    (w)(iii)(A);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within the period set out in one of paragraphs
    26(1)(q) to (s) or subparagraph 26(1)(v)(v) or (w)(v), as the case
    may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in a manner that protects the environment and human
    health against the adverse effects that may result from the
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner of confirmation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The confirmation must be provided in the form and
  manner specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information in confirmation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The confirmation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must identify the reference number of the movement
    document, the line number of the line entry in the movement document
    and the disposal or recycling code set out in column 1 of either
    Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 that applies to the type of hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and the date on which the
    operation was completed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must be dated and signed by the individual who
    provides it and must include that individual's name and telephone
    number.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286865 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  permit holder must keep the following documents that relate to
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is transported
  under one or more permits at their principal place of business in
  Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on which any of
  the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    their compliance with this Division;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every notice that is referred to in subparagraph
    26(1)(k)(v) or subsection 26(3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all contracts that relate to the movement, and the
    disposal or recycling, of the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that they hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported under one or more permits at their principal place of
  business in Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on
  which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the authorized carrier transported the shipment
    in Canada, all documents that are necessary to demonstrate that they
    hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Export from and Import to Canada Following Transit Through a Foreign Country]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1286877 .Subheading}

#### [Permit for Export from and Import to Canada Following Transit Through a Foreign Country]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286878 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** A person may apply for a
permit to export hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material and to
import it to Canada after it is conveyed in transit through a foreign
country if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are an individual who is a resident of Canada or
  a corporation that has its head office, or operates a branch office,
  in Canada, and they

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are the owner or operator of the facility from
    which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
    shipped, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} buy and sell --- or are to buy and sell ---
    hazardous recyclable material for recycling and are to export and
    import such recyclable material for recycling in Canada; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} they are a municipality or Her Majesty in right of
  Canada or a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of application

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be made to the Minister by means of a
  notification that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is
  dated and signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may relate to both hazardous waste and hazardous
    recyclable material;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more shipments that contain
    the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must involve only

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one facility in Canada from which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one receiving facility in Canada to which the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      delivered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 3(i)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 3(i)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 3 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant satisfies the conditions set out in
    section 31;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant and all authorized carriers that are
    to transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
    Canada hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing and manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the
    shipment that contains the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is to be shipped, in the case of a single shipment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the first
    shipment is to be shipped, in the case of multiple shipments.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse
to issue a permit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authorization referred to in subparagraph
  185(1)(b)(i) of the Act is not obtained;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that,
  if the permit were issued, the movement of hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or
  recycling of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit would not
  comply with the Act, these Regulations or conditions to an
  authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of destination or a country
  of transit; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  false or misleading information was provided in the notification under
  section 32.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 36 months after the day on which it is issued, in the
  case of hazardous recyclable material

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is to be conveyed in transit only through a
    country that is subject to OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that is to be recycled by means of a final
    recycling operation at a receiving facility that is a pre-consented
    recovery facility referred to in Case 2 in Chapter II. D (2) of OECD
    Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, as amended from time to time, to the
    extent that the amendments are binding on Canada; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 12 months after the day on which it is issued, in any
  other case.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit if an authority referred to in
  subparagraph 185(1)(b)(i) of the Act withdraws an authorization
  referred to in that subparagraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder no longer satisfies the
    conditions set out in section 31;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit does not comply
    --- or will not comply --- with the Act, these Regulations or the
    conditions of an authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of
    destination or a country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 32;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, cannot be in accordance with the
    permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension or if the permit holder requests the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1286954 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Export from and Import to Canada Following Transit Through a Foreign Country]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286955 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed conditions for the export
of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material and its import to
Canada under one or more permits that are held by a permit holder after
it is conveyed in transit through a foreign country are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the movement, and the disposal or recycling, of the
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be in accordance
  with the permit or permits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time of export,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the export from and import to Canada must not be
    prohibited under the laws of Canada, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the conveyance in transit of the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material through a country must not be
    prohibited by that country;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the permit holder and all authorized carriers that
  transport the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
  Canada must hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
  this Part;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} each shipment that contains hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material must be recorded in a separate movement
  document in accordance with sections 39 and 40;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
  section 40 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
  Minister and the authorities of the provinces where the facility from
  which the shipment was shipped and the receiving facility are located;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
  carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 40, when they
  are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of the shipment
  complies with this Division;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 40, must be
  provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
  be reported at the customs office under section 12 or 95 of the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 41 must be kept
  in accordance with that section.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286968 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped from a facility under one or more permits, Part A
  of the movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment and that was
    recorded as a line entry in the notification under subsection 32(3)
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each line entry must include the reference number
    of the notification, the line entry number and the information that
    was included in the line entry in the notification under subsection
    32(4), with the exception of the information referred to in clauses
    3(i)(iii)(A), (F) and (L) of Schedule 3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in subitem 1(3)
    of Schedule 10 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to Minister and provincial
  authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped from the facility, Part A of the movement document
  must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the Minister to
  the Minister and to the authorities of the provinces where the
  facility and the receiving facility are located, if those authorities
  ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  must complete Part B of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after the shipment is delivered to the
  receiving facility, Part C of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- must be completed as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 3(1) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C to Minister and
  provincial authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the receiving facility, Part B of the
  movement document completed by each authorized carrier and Part C of
  the movement document must be provided, in the form and manner
  specified by the Minister, to the Minister and to the authorities of
  the provinces where the facility from which the shipment was shipped
  and the receiving facility are located, if those authorities ask for
  it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to receiving facility

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) and (5), delivery
  of a shipment to a receiving facility occurs when the shipment arrives
  at the receiving facility, regardless of whether or when hazardous
  waste or hazardous recyclable material that is contained in the
  shipment is accepted by the receiving facility.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1286990 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  permit holder must keep the following documents that relate to
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that is transported
  under one or more permits at their principal place of business in
  Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on which any of
  the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    their compliance with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that they hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported under one or more permits at their principal place of
  business in Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on
  which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the authorized carrier transported the shipment
    in Canada, all documents that are necessary to demonstrate that they
    hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Transit Through Canada]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287000 .Subheading}

#### [Permit for Transit Through Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287001 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit to convey hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material in transit through Canada may be made by any
  person and must be made to the Minister by means of a notification
  that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is dated and
  signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may relate to both hazardous waste and hazardous
    recyclable material;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more shipments that contain
    the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must involve only

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one foreign exporter,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one foreign importer,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one facility in the country of origin from
      which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to
      be shipped, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} one receiving facility in the country of
      destination to which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be delivered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 4(i)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 4(i)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 4 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all authorized carriers that are to transport the
    hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in Canada hold the
    liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing and manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the
    shipment that contains the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is to be shipped, in the case of a single shipment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within 12 months before the day on which the first
    shipment is to be shipped, in the case of multiple shipments.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse
to issue a permit if the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the permit were issued, the transportation of
  hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material under the permit
  would not comply with the Act or these Regulations; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
  notification under section 42.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 36 months after the day on which it is issued, in the
  case of hazardous recyclable material, if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin is subject to OECD Decision
    C(2001)107/FINAL and, if applicable, the hazardous recyclable
    material is to be conveyed in transit only through a country that is
    subject to that Decision, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous recyclable material is to be
    recycled by means of a final recycling operation at a receiving
    facility that is a pre-consented recovery facility referred to in
    Case 2 in Chapter II. D (2) of OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, as
    amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
    binding on Canada; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 12 months after the day on which it is issued, in any
  other case.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit when, or at any time after, a shipment
  enters Canada under the permit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister becomes aware that the competent
    authority of the country of destination or of a country through
    which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
    conveyed in transit after it has left Canada has not authorized the
    import or conveyance in transit, as the case may be; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the competent authority of one of those countries
    withdraws its authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 42;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit will not comply
    with the Act, these Regulations or the conditions of an
    authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of destination or a
    country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material under the permit does not comply with the Act or
    these Regulations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the transportation of hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material cannot be in accordance with the
    permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension or if the permit holder requests the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287073 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Transit Through Canada]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287074 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed conditions for the
conveyance of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
transit through Canada under one or more permits that are held by a
permit holder are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material must be in accordance with the permit or
  permits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time the shipment that contains the hazardous
  waste or hazardous recyclable material enters Canada, the conveyance
  in transit of the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  through Canada must not be prohibited under the laws of Canada;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the time the shipment enters Canada, the competent
  authority of the country of destination and of every country through
  which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
  conveyed in transit after it has left Canada must have authorized the
  import or conveyance in transit, as the case may be;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} all authorized carriers that transport the hazardous
  waste or hazardous recyclable material in Canada must hold the
  liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} each shipment must be recorded in a separate movement
  document in accordance with sections 49 and 50;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
  section 50 must be provided to the Minister in accordance with that
  section;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment in Canada, every
  authorized carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and
  B of the movement document completed in accordance with section 50,
  when they are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of
  the shipment complies with this Division;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 50, must be
  provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
  be reported at the customs office under section 12 or 95 of the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 51 must be kept
  in accordance with that section.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287086 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material enters Canada under one or more permits, Part A of the
  movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 1(4) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  in Canada must complete Part B of the movement document --- with Part
  A completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts A and B to Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  in Canada must provide the movement document --- of which Parts A and
  B are completed in accordance with subsection (1) and (2) --- to the
  Minister, in the form and manner specified by the Minister, within
  three working days after the shipment leaves Canada.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287098 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  permit holder that has a place of business in Canada must keep all
  documents that relate to hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material transported under one or more permits and that are necessary
  to demonstrate their compliance with this Division at their principal
  place of business in Canada for a period of five years after the
  latest day on which any of the permits expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported in Canada under one or more permits at their
  principal place of business in Canada for a period of five years after
  the latest day on which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that they hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of
    this Part.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Return to Canada]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287106 .Subheading}

#### [Permit for Return to Canada]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287107 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When holder of export permit or permits
must apply

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the
  circumstances set out in clauses 26(1)(v)(iii)(B) and (w)(iii)(B), the
  holder of the export permit or permits must apply for an import permit
  to return to Canada hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  that was exported from Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other cases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In any other case, a person in Canada may apply for
  an import permit to return to Canada hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that was exported from Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of application

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be made to the Minister by means of a
  notification that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is
  dated and signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material that were exported from Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must involve only one shipment for the return.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 5(j)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 5(j)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 5 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse
to issue a permit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authorization referred to in subparagraph
  185(1)(b)(i) of the Act is not obtained from a country of transit,
  unless the circumstances in subsection 185(4) of the Act apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that,
  if the permit were issued, the movement of hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material under the permit would not comply with
  the Act, these Regulations or conditions to an authorization imposed
  by a country of transit; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  false or misleading information was provided in the notification under
  section 53.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than 12 months after the
day on which it is issued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit if an authority of a country of transit
  withdraws an authorization referred to in subparagraph 185(1)(b)(i) of
  the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 53;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit will not comply
    with the Act, these Regulations or the conditions of an
    authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of destination or a
    country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material under the permit does not comply with the Act,
    these Regulations or conditions to an authorization imposed by a
    country of transit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material cannot be in accordance with the permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287170 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Return to Canada]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287171 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed conditions for the return
of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to Canada under one
or more permits that are held by a permit holder are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the movement of the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material must be in accordance with the permit or permits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the conveyance in transit of the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material through a country must not be prohibited
  by that country;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the shipment that contains the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material must be recorded in a movement document
  in accordance with sections 60 and 61;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
  section 61 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
  Minister and the authorities of the province where the facility to
  which the shipment is to be delivered is located;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
  carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 61, when they
  are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of the shipment
  complies with this Division;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 61, must be
  provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
  be reported at the customs office under section 12 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the shipment must be delivered to the facility in
  Canada from which the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  was originally shipped unless another facility in Canada is identified
  in the permit or permits, in which case the shipment must be delivered
  to that facility; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 62 must be kept
  in accordance with that section.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287182 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped from a facility under one or more permits, Part A
  of the movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment and that was
    recorded as a line entry in the notification under subsection 53(3)
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each line entry must include the reference number
    of the notification, the line entry number and the information that
    was included in the line entry in the notification under subsection
    53(4) --- with the exception of the information referred to in
    clauses 5(j)(iii)(A), (E) and (K) of Schedule 3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in subitem 1(5)
    of Schedule 10 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to Minister and provincial
  authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped from the facility, Part A of the movement document
  must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the Minister, to
  the Minister and to the authorities of the province where the facility
  to which the shipment is to be delivered is located, if those
  authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  must complete Part B of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after the shipment is delivered to the
  facility to which the shipment is shipped under the permit or permits,
  Part C of the movement document --- with Part A completed in
  accordance with subsection (1) --- must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 3(2) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C to Minister and
  provincial authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the facility, Part B of the movement document
  completed by each authorized carrier and Part C of the movement
  document must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the
  Minister, to the Minister and to the authorities of the province where
  the facility is located, if those authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to Part C

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Part C of the movement document may be amended only
  with respect to the quantities specified in it. The amendments must be
  made in the form and manner specified by the Minister and within 180
  days after the day on which the shipment is delivered to the facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to facility

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) to (6), delivery
  of a shipment to a facility occurs when the shipment arrives at the
  facility, regardless of when the facility acknowledges receipt of the
  shipment.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287206 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  permit holder must keep all documents that relate to hazardous waste
  or hazardous recyclable material transported under one or more permits
  and that are necessary to demonstrate their compliance with this
  Division at their principal place of business in Canada for a period
  of five years after the latest day on which any of the permits
  expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported under one or more permits at their principal place of
  business in Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on
  which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the authorized carrier transported the shipment
    in Canada, all documents that are necessary to demonstrate that they
    hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part.

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Return to Foreign Country of Origin]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287214 .Subheading}

#### [Permit for Return to Foreign Country of Origin]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287215 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When holder of import permit or permits
must apply

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the
  circumstances set out in clauses 14(1)(u)(iii)(B) and (v)(iii)(B), the
  holder of the import permit or permits must apply for an export permit
  to return to the country of origin hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that was imported to Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other cases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In any other case, a person in Canada may apply for
  an export permit to return to the country of origin hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material that was imported to Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of application
