- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be made to the Minister by means of a
  notification that is in the form specified by the Minister and that is
  dated and signed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notification

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may relate to one or more types of hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material that were imported to Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must involve only one shipment for the return.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  must be categorized into separate types by reference to all of the
  information set out in subparagraphs 6(j)(i) to (iii) of Schedule 3
  and each type for which all of that information is the same must be
  recorded as a line entry in the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information in notification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information set out in subparagraphs 6(j)(i)
  to (iii) of Schedule 3 must be included in each line entry and the
  remaining information set out in item 6 of that Schedule must be
  included in the notification along with a certification, which is
  dated and signed by the individual who provides the information, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant will ensure that the hazardous waste
    or hazardous recyclable material is managed in a manner that
    protects the environment and human health against the adverse
    effects that may result from it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information provided is accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The notification must be made in the manner specified
  by the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to issue permit

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must refuse
to issue a permit if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authorization referred to in subparagraph
  185(1)(b)(i) of the Act is not obtained from a country of transit,
  unless the circumstances in subsection 185(4) of the Act apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that,
  if the permit were issued, the movement of hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material under the permit would not comply with
  the Act, these Regulations or conditions to an authorization imposed
  by a country of transit; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that
  false or misleading information was provided in the notification under
  section 64.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of permit

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** A permit is valid for the
period set out in it, which cannot be longer than 12 months after the
day on which it is issued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New application

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** If a permit needs to be
amended the permit holder must apply for a new permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of permit required

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend a permit if an authority of a country of transit
  withdraws an authorization referred to in subparagraph 185(1)(b)(i) of
  the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional suspension of permit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may suspend a permit if the Minister has
  reasonable grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} false or misleading information was provided in the
    notification under section 64;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, or the disposal of hazardous waste or recycling
    of hazardous recyclable material, under the permit will not comply
    with the Act, these Regulations or the conditions of an
    authorization imposed by the jurisdiction of destination or a
    country of transit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material under the permit does not comply with the Act,
    these Regulations or conditions to an authorization imposed by a
    country of transit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as a result of a natural or artificial disaster or
    another emergency, the movement of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material cannot be in accordance with the permit; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material is not being managed in a manner that will protect the
    environment and human health against the adverse effects that may
    result from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the permit holder of the
  suspension, the reasons for it and the period during which it applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the suspension and the
    reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should be reinstated within the
  period of the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of permit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may reinstate the permit only if the
  matter that gave rise to the suspension has been resolved or corrected
  during the period of the suspension.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of permit

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke a permit if the matter that gave rise to a
  suspension has not been resolved or corrected within the period of
  suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must notify
  the permit holder of the proposed revocation and the reasons for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister notifies the permit holder when the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informs the permit holder at their last known
    address or email address that the notice of the proposed revocation
    and the reasons for it are available through a generally accessible
    electronic source and identifies the location of the notice and
    reasons; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends the notice and reasons to the permit holder's
    last known address or email address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit holder may make written representations to
  the Minister as to why the permit should not be revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is not effective until 20
  days after the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

#### [Conditions Under Paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1287278 .Subheading}

##### [Conditions Relating to Return to Foreign Country of Origin]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287279 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 185(1)(c) of the Act, the prescribed conditions for the return
of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to the country of
origin under one or more permits that are held by a permit holder are
the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the movement of the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material must be in accordance with the permit or permits;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the conveyance in transit of the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material through a country must not be prohibited
  by that country;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the shipment that contains the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material must be recorded in a movement document
  in accordance with sections 71 and 72;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
  section 72 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
  Minister and the authorities of the province where the facility from
  which the shipment is shipped is located;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
  carrier must produce the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 72, when they
  are required to do so to demonstrate that the movement of the shipment
  complies with this Division;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the permit or permits, and Parts A and B of the
  movement document completed in accordance with section 72, must be
  provided to a customs office when the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is required to
  be reported at the customs office under section 95 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the shipment must be delivered to the facility in the
  country of origin from which the hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material was originally shipped unless another facility in
  the country of origin is identified in the permit or permits, in which
  case the shipment must be delivered to that facility; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 73 must be kept
  in accordance with that section.

##### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287290 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit of measure

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** The unit of measure set
out in the movement document to describe the quantity of a type of
hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material must be the same as
that used in the applicable permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped from a facility under one or more permits, Part A
  of the movement document must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is contained in the shipment and that was
    recorded as a line entry in the notification under subsection 64(3)
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each line entry must include the reference number
    of the notification, the line entry number and the information that
    was included in the line entry in the notification under subsection
    64(4), with the exception of the information referred to in clauses
    6(j)(iii)(A), (E) and (K) of Schedule 3;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in subitem 1(6)
    of Schedule 10 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to Minister and provincial
  authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped from the facility, Part A of the movement document
  must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the Minister, to
  the Minister and to the authorities of the province where the facility
  is located, if those authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that transports the shipment
  must complete Part B of the movement document --- with Part A
  completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including the
  information set out in item 2 of Schedule 10 and a certification that
  the information provided is accurate and complete. The individual who
  provides the information and certification must date and sign Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Immediately after the shipment is delivered to the
  facility to which the shipment is shipped under the permit or permits,
  Part C of the movement document --- with Part A completed in
  accordance with subsection (1) --- must be completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subitem 3(2) of Schedule
    10 and a certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete must be included; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C to Minister and
  provincial authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the facility, Part B of the movement document
  completed by each authorized carrier and Part C of the movement
  document must be provided, in the form and manner specified by the
  Minister, to the Minister and to the authorities of the province where
  the facility from which the shipment was shipped is located, if those
  authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to Part C

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Part C of the movement document may be amended only
  with respect to the quantities specified in it. The amendments must be
  made in the form and manner specified by the Minister and within 180
  days after the day on which the shipment is delivered to the facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to facility

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) to (6), delivery
  of a shipment to a facility occurs when the shipment arrives at the
  facility, regardless of when the facility acknowledges receipt of the
  shipment.

##### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText4} {#h-1287314 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permit holder

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  permit holder must keep all documents that relate to hazardous waste
  or hazardous recyclable material transported under one or more permits
  and that are necessary to demonstrate their compliance with this
  Division at their principal place of business in Canada for a period
  of five years after the latest day on which any of the permits
  expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier that has a place of business
  in Canada must keep the following documents that relate to a shipment
  that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  they transported under one or more permits at their principal place of
  business in Canada for a period of five years after the latest day on
  which any of the permits expires:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
    that the movement of the shipment complies with this Division; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the authorized carrier transported the shipment
    in Canada, all documents that are necessary to demonstrate that they
    hold the liability insurance referred to in Division 7 of this Part.

### [DIVISION 7]{.HLabel2}[Liability Insurance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287322 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application to the Crown

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** This Division does not
apply to Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of insurance --- permit holder

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  amount of liability insurance to be held by a permit holder referred
  to in any of Divisions 1 to 3, 5 and 6 of this Part is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least \$5,000,000 in respect of a shipment that
    contains hazardous waste; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least \$1,000,000 in respect of a shipment that
    contains hazardous recyclable material.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of insurance --- authorized
  carrier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of liability insurance to be held by an
  authorized carrier that transports a shipment that contains hazardous
  waste or hazardous recyclable material in Canada is the amount
  required under federal or provincial legislation that applies to it in
  respect of the transport of that shipment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coverage

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** The liability insurance
to be held by a permit holder must be in respect of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} damages to third parties for which the permit holder
  is responsible; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} costs imposed by law on the permit holder to clean up
  the environment in respect of any hazardous waste or hazardous
  recyclable material that is released.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of coverage

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** The insurance must cover
liability that arises

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a permit holder that imports a shipment under
  Division 1 of this Part, from the time that the shipment enters Canada
  until all of the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material that
  is contained in the shipment is accepted by the receiving facility,
  or, in the circumstances set out in paragraph 14(1)(u) or (v), until
  it either is accepted by an authorized facility in Canada that is to
  perform the final disposal operation or final recycling operation or
  is shipped from a facility in Canada for return under Division 6 of
  this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a permit holder that exports a shipment under
  Division 2 of this Part, from the time that the shipment is shipped
  from a facility in Canada until all of the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is
  accepted by the receiving facility or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the circumstances set out in paragraph 26(1)(v),
    until it either is accepted by an authorized facility in the country
    of destination that is to perform the final disposal operation or
    final recycling operation or is shipped under Division 5 of this
    Part from a facility for return to Canada, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the circumstances set out in paragraph
    26(1)(w), until it either is accepted by an authorized facility that
    is located in the applicable country of destination or transit and
    that is to perform the final disposal operation or final recycling
    operation or is shipped under Division 5 of this Part from a
    facility for return to Canada;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a permit holder that, under Division 3 of this
  Part, exports a shipment and imports it to Canada after it is conveyed
  in transit through a foreign country, from the time that the shipment
  is shipped from a facility in Canada until all of the hazardous waste
  or hazardous recyclable material that is contained in the shipment is
  accepted by an authorized facility in Canada;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} for a permit holder that imports a shipment for
  return to Canada under Division 5 of this Part, from the time that the
  shipment is shipped from a facility in the foreign country until it
  arrives at the facility in Canada to which it is shipped under that
  Division;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} for a permit holder that exports a shipment for
  return to a foreign country of origin under Division 6 of this Part,
  from the time that the shipment is shipped from a facility in Canada
  until it leaves Canada; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} for an authorized carrier, for the duration of the
  transportation in Canada.

### [DIVISION 8]{.HLabel2}[Export Reduction Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287346 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of plan

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  plan referred to in subsection 188(1) of the Act must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste to which the plan
    applies,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the disposal code set out in column 1 of Part 1
        of Schedule 1 for the applicable operation set out in column 2
        of that Part to be performed at the receiving facility,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste is a
        liquid, the letter P when it is a sludge, the letter S when it
        is a solid and the letter G when it is a gas, followed in each
        case by the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part 2 of
        Schedule 11,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that produces a
        leachate that contains one or more environmentally hazardous
        constituents, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1
        of Schedule 2 for each environmentally hazardous constituent,
        and

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the exporter generates the hazardous waste
    referred to in the plan, the name and description of the process
    that generates the hazardous waste and of the activity in which that
    process is used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the origin of the hazardous waste if the exporter
    does not generate it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of hazardous waste exported at the
    start of the implementation of the plan and the quantity of export
    reduction to be achieved at each stage of the plan;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of how the exporter will reduce or
    phase out exports of the hazardous waste referred to in the plan;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the options considered for reducing or phasing out
    exports of the hazardous waste referred to in the plan, including
    options for disposing of it in Canada;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the stages of the plan and a schedule for
    completing each stage; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for each stage of the plan, an estimate of the
    quantity, in kilograms or litres, of goods produced that generate
    the hazardous waste referred to in the plan and a description of the
    impact of any changes to the quantity produced on the reduction or
    phasing out of exports of that hazardous waste.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of plan

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who submits a plan to the Minister must keep
  a copy of the plan at their principal place of business in Canada for
  a period of five years after the day on which the last stage of the
  plan is completed.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Movement Within Canada]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1287374 .Part}

### [Conditions Relating to Movement Within Canada]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287375 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 189(1) of the Act, the movement by a person of hazardous
waste or hazardous recyclable material within Canada is subject to the
following conditions:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shipment that contains the hazardous waste or
  hazardous recyclable material must be shipped by a consignor to a site
  that is owned, operated or otherwise controlled by a consignee;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the shipment must be recorded in a movement document
  in accordance with section 80;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the movement document completed in accordance with
  section 80 must be provided, in accordance with that section, to the
  consignor and to the authorities of the provinces from which the
  shipment is shipped and to which it is delivered;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the shipment must be transported only by authorized
  carriers;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} while they transport the shipment, every authorized
  carrier must produce Parts A and B of the movement document completed
  in accordance with section 80 when they are required to do so to
  demonstrate that the movement of the shipment complies with this Part;
  and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the documents referred to in section 81 must be kept
  in accordance with that section.

### [Movement Document]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287384 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material is shipped by a consignor, the consignor must ensure that
  Part A of the movement document is completed as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
    material must be categorized into separate types by reference to all
    of the information set out in subparagraphs 1(g)(i) and (ii) of
    Schedule 4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material for which all of that information is the same
    must be recorded as a line entry;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in paragraph 1(g) of
    Schedule 4 must be included in each line entry;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the remaining information set out in item 1 of
    Schedule 4 and a certification that the information provided is
    accurate and complete must be included; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification must date and sign Part A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part A to provincial authorities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped, the consignor must ensure that Part A of the
  movement document is provided, in the form and manner specified by the
  Minister, to the authorities of the provinces from which the shipment
  is shipped and to which it is or is to be delivered, if those
  authorities ask for it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part B

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} complete Part B of the movement document --- with
    Part A completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- by including
    the information set out in item 2 of Schedule 4 and a certification
    that the information provided is accurate and complete; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the individual who provides the
    information and certification dates and signs Part B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part C

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Within three working days after the day on which the
  shipment is delivered to the site, the consignee must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Part C of the movement document --- with Part A
    completed in accordance with subsection (1) --- is completed by
    including the information set out in item 3 of Schedule 4 and a
    certification that the information provided is accurate and
    complete;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual who provides the information and
    certification dates and signs Part C; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Part B of the movement document completed by each
    authorized carrier and Part C of the movement document are provided
    to the consignor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parts B and C --- provincial
  authorities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The consignee must ensure that Part B completed by
  each authorized carrier and Part C of the movement document are
  provided to the authorities of the provinces from which the shipment
  is shipped and to which it is delivered, if those authorities ask for
  it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to site

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), delivery of a
  shipment to the site occurs when the shipment arrives at the site,
  regardless of when hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  that is contained in the shipment is accepted by the consignee.

### [Record Retention]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287408 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consignor

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  consignor must keep all documents that are necessary to demonstrate
  their compliance with this Part at their principal place of business
  in Canada for a period of five years after the day on which the
  shipment is shipped.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consignee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The consignee must keep all documents that are
  necessary to demonstrate their compliance with this Part at their
  principal place of business in Canada for a period of five years after
  the day on which the shipment is delivered to the site.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized carrier

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each authorized carrier must keep all documents that
  are necessary to demonstrate that the movement of the shipment
  complies with this Part at their principal place of business in Canada
  for a period of five years after the day on which they took possession
  of the shipment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to site

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), delivery of a
  shipment to the site occurs when the shipment arrives at the site,
  regardless of when hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
  that is contained in the shipment is accepted by the consignee.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Consequential Amendments, Transitional Provision, Repeals and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1287418 .Part}

### [Transitional Provision]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287448 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Previous notices and permits

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** If a notice is submitted
to the Minister as an application for a permit under the [Export and
Import of Hazardous Waste and Hazardous Recyclable Material
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2005-149), or a permit is issued in
connection with such a notice, those Regulations, as they read
immediately before the coming into force of these Regulations, apply to
the notice or the permit and to the movement, and the disposal or
recycling, of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material under the
permit.

### [Repeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1287451 .Subheading}

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** The following Regulations
are repealed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [PCB Waste Export Regulations,
  1996](/eng/regulations/SOR-97-109)[[Footnote

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [Interprovincial Movement of Hazardous Waste

  and

  - ::: Footnote

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Export and Import of Hazardous Waste and

## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 1, paragraphs 2(1)(d) and 4(1)(d), clause 78(1)(a)(iii)(J) and Schedules 3 and 4)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1287476 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="1287477">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

## [SCHEDULE 3]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 1, subsections 8(3) and (4), paragraph 16(1)(b), subsections 20(3) and (4), paragraph 28(1)(b), subsections 32(3) and (4), paragraph 40(1)(b), subsections 42(3) and (4) and 53(3) and (4), paragraph 61(1)(b), subsections 64(3) and (4) and paragraph 72(1)(b))]{.OriginatingRef}[Notification for Import, Export and Transit Permits --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1287484 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information is required in a notification for an
  import permit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's civic address in Canada, which, in
    the case of a corporation, is the address of its head office or
    branch office in Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the foreign exporter's name, mailing address and
    unique identification number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the foreign exporter,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation,
      and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    section 12 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} each country of transit; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code set out in
        column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 for the applicable
        operation set out in column 2 of that Part to be performed at
        the receiving facility,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is either to be imported from a country of origin, or
        conveyed in transit through a country, that is subject to OECD
        Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code, if any, set out
        in Part II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as amended from time
        to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(L)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of that type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to
      be shipped under the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the proposed number of shipments of that type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to be shipped
      under the permit,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code for the final
      disposal operation or final recycling operation that is to be
      performed on that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material by an authorized facility other than the receiving
      facility and the name of the authorized facility that is to
      perform that operation, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- **2** The following information is required in a notification for an
  export permit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's civic address in Canada, which, in
    the case of a corporation, is the address of its head office or
    branch office in Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer's name, mailing address and
    unique identification number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation,
      and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    section 95 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} each country of transit; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code set out in
        column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 for the applicable
        operation set out in column 2 of that Part to be performed at
        the receiving facility,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is either to be exported to a country of destination, or
        conveyed in transit through a country, that is subject to OECD
        Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code, if any, set out
        in Part II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as amended from time
        to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(L)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of that type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to
      be shipped under the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the proposed number of shipments of that type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to be shipped
      under the permit,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code for the final
      disposal operation or final recycling operation that is to be
      performed on that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material by an authorized facility other than the receiving
      facility and the name of the authorized facility that is to
      perform that operation, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- **3** The following information is required in a notification for a
  permit to export hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material and
  to import it to Canada after it is conveyed in transit through a
  foreign country:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's civic address in Canada, which, in
    the case of a corporation, is the address of its head office or
    branch office in Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} every other authorized facility that is to
      perform a final disposal operation or final recycling operation,
      and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    sections 12 and 95 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) or an
    indication that one or both of those sections do not apply;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each country of transit; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code set out in
        column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 for the applicable
        operation set out in column 2 of that Part to be performed at
        the receiving facility,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is to be conveyed in transit through a country that is
        subject to OECD Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code,
        if any, set out in Part II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(L)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of that type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to
      be shipped under the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the proposed number of shipments of that type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to be shipped
      under the permit,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code for the final
      disposal operation or final recycling operation that is to be
      performed on that type of hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material by an authorized facility other than the receiving
      facility and the name of the authorized facility that is to
      perform that operation, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- **4** The following information is required in a notification for a
  permit to convey hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in
  transit through Canada:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, civic address --- and, if different, mailing address ---
    and unique identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer's name, mailing address and
    unique identification number;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material in Canada and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the facility from which the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material in Canada;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    sections 12 and 95 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) or an
    indication that one or both of those sections do not apply;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each foreign country of transit; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the disposal or recycling code set out in
        column 1 of either Part 1 or 2 of Schedule 1 for the applicable
        operation set out in column 2 of that Part to be performed at
        the receiving facility,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is either to be exported to a country of destination, or
        conveyed in transit through a country, that is subject to OECD
        Decision C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code, if any, set out
        in Part II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as amended from time
        to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(L)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of that type of hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to
      be shipped under the permit,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the proposed number of shipments of that type of
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material to be shipped
      under the permit, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- **5** The following information is required in a notification for an
  import permit to return hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material to Canada:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is the permit holder referred to
    in subsection 52(1),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the reference number of the movement document or
      movement documents for the shipment of the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material under the original export permit or
      permits,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} every line entry in that movement document or
      movement documents that applies to the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be returned to Canada,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique
      identification number of the foreign importer that is named in the
      original export permit or permits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's civic address in Canada, which, in
    the case of a corporation, is the address of its head office or
    branch office in Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility in the foreign country from which
      the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      shipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the facility in Canada to which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be delivered;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the foreign importer that is
      named in the original export permit or permits,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the facility in the foreign country from which
      the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      shipped,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the facility in Canada to which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be delivered, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    section 12 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} each country of transit; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is either to be returned from, or conveyed in transit
        through, a country that is subject to OECD Decision
        C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code, if any, set out in Part
        II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as amended from time to time,
        to the extent that the amendments are binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of that type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to be
      returned under the permit, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

- **6** The following information is required in a notification for an
  export permit to return hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
  material to a foreign country of origin:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
    Minister for the notification;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant is the permit holder referred to
    in subsection 63(1),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the reference number of the movement document or
      movement documents for the shipment of the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material under the original import permit or
      permits,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} every line entry in that movement document or
      movement documents that applies to the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material that is to be returned to the
      country of origin, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique
      identification number of the foreign exporter that is named in the
      original import permit or permits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant's civic address in Canada, which, in
    the case of a corporation, is the address of its head office or
    branch office in Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, civic address and unique identification
    number of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the facility in Canada from which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the facility in the country of origin to which
      the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      delivered;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name, mailing address and unique identification
    number of each authorized carrier that is to transport the hazardous
    waste or hazardous recyclable material and each mode of
    transportation that is to be used by them;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number and email address of the
    contact person for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the foreign exporter that is named in the
      original import permit or permits,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the facility in Canada from which the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be shipped,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the facility in the country of origin to which
      the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material is to be
      delivered, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} each authorized carrier that is to transport the
      hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} each customs office at which the hazardous waste or
    hazardous recyclable material is required to be reported under
    section 95 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} each country of transit; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
    notification,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 1 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 3 of Schedule 11 or C0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11 or H0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code set out in column 1 of Part
        5 of Schedule 11,

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in Annex I or II
        to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as amended from
        time to time, to the extent that the amendments are binding on
        Canada or Y0 when no code is applicable,

      - [(G)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in List A
        of Annex VIII to the Convention, as it read on May 5, 1992, as
        amended from time to time, to the extent that the amendments are
        binding on Canada,

      - [(H)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous recyclable material
        that is either to be returned to, or conveyed in transit
        through, a country that is subject to OECD Decision
        C(2001)107/FINAL, the applicable code, if any, set out in Part
        II of Appendix 4 to that Decision, as amended from time to time,
        to the extent that the amendments are binding on Canada,

      - [(I)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(J)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(K)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of that type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to be
      shipped under the permit, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the quantity and concentration of every
      persistent organic pollutant that is contained in the hazardous
      waste or hazardous recyclable material.

## [SCHEDULE 4]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 1 and paragraphs 80(1)(a), (c) and (d), (3)(a) and (4)(a))]{.OriginatingRef}[Movement Document for Movement Within Canada --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1287711 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following information is required before a shipment is
  shipped from a consignor to a site that is owned, operated or
  otherwise controlled by a consignee:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided for the
    movement document by the Minister or the province from which the
    shipment is to be shipped or to which it is to be delivered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consignor's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual who
    signs the movement document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address --- or, if there is no civic
    address, the Global Positioning System (GPS) coordinates --- of the
    final loading point of the shipment before crossing the provincial
    border;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the consignee's name, telephone number, email
    address and unique identification number;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address --- or, if there is no civic
    address, the location --- of the site to which the shipment is to be
    delivered;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the movement
    document,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicable UN number set out in column 1 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) or, if no UN number
      applies to it under those Regulations, the abbreviation "n/a",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
      out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous
      Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and are
      associated with the UN number referred to in that line entry,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable class set out in column 3 of
      Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of Dangerous Goods
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the following codes:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the letter L when the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material is a liquid, the letter P when it
        is a sludge, the letter S when it is a solid and the letter G
        when it is a gas, followed in each case by the applicable code
        set out in column 1 of Part 2 of Schedule 11,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the applicable codes set out in column 1 of
        Part 4 of Schedule 11,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste that is set out
        in column 2 of Schedule 6 or 12 or column 3 of Schedule 8 or
        that contains a substance that is set out in column 3 of
        Schedule 7, the applicable codes, if any, set out in column 1 of
        the applicable Schedule,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} in the case of hazardous waste or hazardous
        recyclable material that produces a leachate that contains one
        or more environmentally hazardous constituents, the applicable
        codes, if any, set out in column 1 of Schedule 2 for each
        environmentally hazardous constituent, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the applicable code, if any, set out in column
        1 of Schedule 5 for every persistent organic pollutant that is
        contained in the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of that type of hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, to be
      shipped, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
      and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
      tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable material
      is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton, 05 when it
      is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when it is
      palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
      container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
      case; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped and
    the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered.

- **2** The following information is required from an authorized
  carrier:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the authorized carrier's name, telephone number,
    email address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual who
    signs the movement document; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a confirmation

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the authorized carrier has received the
      shipment, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, that they are to deliver the
      shipment to the site that is owned, operated or otherwise
      controlled by the consignee.

- **3** The following information is required after the shipment is
  delivered:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the consignee's name, telephone number, email
    address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual who
    signs the movement document;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the civic address --- or, if there is no civic
    address, the location --- of the site to which the shipment was
    delivered;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment was delivered; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the movement
    document, the quantity of the hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, that was received.
::::
