## [SCHEDULE 6]{.scheduleLabel}[(Paragraphs 2(1)(a), 4(1)(a), 14(1)(e) and 26(1)(f), clause 78(1)(a)(iii)(I) and Schedules 3 and 4)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1287754 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="1287755">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Code</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Description of Hazardous
Waste and Hazardous Recyclable Material</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> Biomedical waste — the
following waste, other than that set out in subitem (2) or that
generated from building maintenance, office administration or food
preparation and consumption, that is generated by human or animal health
care establishments, medical, health care or veterinary teaching or
research establishments, clinical laboratories or facilities that test
or produce vaccines, and needle and syringe exchange programs:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> human tissues, organs or body
parts, excluding teeth, hair or nails;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> human blood or blood
products;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> human bodily fluids that are
contaminated with blood;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> human bodily fluids that are
removed in the course of autopsy, treatment, or surgery for
diagnosis;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> animal tissues, organs, body
parts or carcasses, excluding teeth, nails, hair, bristles, feathers,
horns and hooves, that result from the treatment of an animal for
contamination or suspected contamination with an infectious substance
that meets the criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2
of the <a href="/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286">Transportation of
Dangerous Goods Regulations</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> animal blood or blood
products that result from the treatment of an animal for contamination
or suspected contamination with an infectious substance that meets the
criteria for inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the <a
href="/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286">Transportation of Dangerous Goods
Regulations</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> animal bodily fluids that are
visibly contaminated with animal blood and that result from the
treatment of an animal for contamination or suspected contamination with
an infectious substance that meets the criteria for inclusion in Class
6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the <a
href="/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286">Transportation of Dangerous Goods
Regulations</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> animal bodily fluids that are
removed in the course of surgery, treatment or necropsy, and that result
from the treatment of an animal for contamination or suspected
contamination with an infectious substance that meets the criteria for
inclusion in Class 6.2 as set out in Part 2 of the <a
href="/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286">Transportation of Dangerous Goods
Regulations</a>;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> live or attenuated vaccines,
human or animal cell cultures, microbiology laboratory cultures, stocks
or specimens of microorganisms and any items that have come into contact
with them;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> any items that are saturated
with the blood or bodily fluids referred to in paragraphs (b) to (d) or
(f) to (h), including items that were saturated but have dried;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> cytotoxic drugs and any
items, including tissues, tubing, needles or gloves, that have come into
contact with a cytotoxic drug.</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> Biomedical waste does not
include the following:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> urine or feces;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> waste that is controlled
under the <a href="/eng/acts/H-3.3">Health of Animals Act</a>;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> waste that results from the
breeding or raising of animals.</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Used lubricating oils in quantities of 500 L
or more, from internal combustion engines or gear boxes, transmissions,
transformers, hydraulic systems or other equipment associated with such
engines.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ3</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Used oil filters containing more than 6% of
oil by mass.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ4</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Cyanide, or substances containing cyanide, in
a concentration equal to or greater than 100 mg/kg.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ5</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste that contains more than 2 mg/kg of
polychlorinated terphenyls or polybrominated biphenyls as those
substances are described in Schedule 1 to the Act.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ6</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> Waste that contains, in a
concentration of more than 100 ng/kg of
2,3,7,8-tetrachlorodibenzo-p-dioxin equivalent, calculated in accordance
with subitem (2),</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> total polychlorinated
dibenzofurans that have a molecular formula
C<sub>12</sub>H<sub>8-n</sub>Cl<sub>n</sub>O in which “n” is greater
than 1; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> total polychlorinated
dibenzo-p-dioxins that have a molecular formula
C<sub>12</sub>H<sub>8-n</sub>Cl<sub>n</sub>O<sub>2</sub> in which “n” is
greater than 1.</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> The concentration is
calculated in accordance with “International Toxicity Equivalency Factor
(I-TEF) Method of Risk Assessment for Complex Mixtures of Dioxins and
Related Compounds”, Pilot Study on International Information Exchange on
Dioxins and Related Compounds, Committee on the Challenges of Modern
Society, North Atlantic Treaty Organization, Report Number 176, August
1988, as amended from time to time, using the following factors:</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(a)</span> 2,3,7,8-tetrachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />1.0</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(b)</span> 1,2,3,7,8-pentachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.5</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(c)</span> 1,2,3,4,7,8-hexachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(d)</span> 1,2,3,7,8,9-hexachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(e)</span> 1,2,3,6,7,8-hexachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(f)</span> 1,2,3,4,6,7,8-heptachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.01</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> octachlorodibenzodioxin<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.001</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(h)</span> 2,3,7,8-tetrachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(i)</span> 2,3,4,7,8-pentachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.5</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(j)</span> 1,2,3,7,8-pentachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.05</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(k)</span> 1,2,3,4,7,8-hexachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(l)</span> 1,2,3,7,8,9-hexachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(m)</span> 1,2,3,6,7,8-hexachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(n)</span> 2,3,4,6,7,8-hexachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.1</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(o)</span> 1,2,3,4,6,7,8-heptachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.01</p></li>
<li><p><span
class="tablelabel">(p)</span> 1,2,3,4,7,8,9-heptachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.01</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(q)</span> octachlorodibenzofuran<img
src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified"
alt="blank line" />0.001</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">HAZ7</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Circuit boards and display devices and any
equipment that contains them.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">HAZ8</td>
<td>Non-rechargeable and rechargeable cells and batteries.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## [SCHEDULE 9]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subparagraphs 4(2)(g)(i) and (3)(g)(i))]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1287786 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="1287787">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="borderBottom top" scope="col">Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Slags, skimmings and dross containing
precious metals, copper or zinc for further refining.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Platinum group metal (PGM) automobile
catalysts.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Any part of electronic equipment — including
scrap but excluding cells and batteries — that is suitable for base or
precious metal recovery.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">4</td>
<td>Brass in the form of turnings, borings and choppings.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>Excluded Material</p></figcaption>
</figure>

## [SCHEDULE 10]{.scheduleLabel}[(Paragraph 16(1)(c), subsection 16(3), paragraphs 16(4)(a) and 28(1)(c), subsection 28(3), paragraphs 28(4)(a) and 40(1)(c), subsection 40(3), paragraphs 40(4)(a) and 50(1)(a), subsection 50(2), paragraph 61(1)(c), subsection 61(3), paragraphs 61(4)(a) and 72(1)(c), subsection 72(3) and paragraph 72(4)(a))]{.OriginatingRef}[Movement Document for Imports, Exports and Transits --- Information Required]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1287794 .scheduleLabel}
:::

#### [Movement Document --- Part A]{.HTitleText2} {#movement-document-part-a .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an import, the following
    information is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the import permit or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the foreign exporter's name, telephone number,
      email address, mailing address and unique identification number;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the receiving facility to
      which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped
      and the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered to the
      receiving facility.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an export, the following information
    is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the export permit or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer's name, telephone number,
      email address, mailing address and unique identification number;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the receiving facility to
      which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped
      and the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered to the
      receiving facility.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an export from and import to Canada
    following transit through a foreign country, the following
    information is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the permit or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the receiving facility to
      which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped
      and the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered to the
      receiving facility.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a transit through Canada, the
    following information is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the transit permit or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the foreign importer's name, telephone number,
      email address, mailing address and unique identification number;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the receiving facility to
      which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped,
      the date on which it is scheduled to enter Canada and the date on
      which it is scheduled to be delivered to the receiving facility.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a return to Canada, the following
    information is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the permit holder is the holder of the
      original export permit or permits, the name, telephone number,
      email address, mailing address and unique identification number of
      the foreign importer that is named in the original export permit
      or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped for return;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the import permit for the return;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility in Canada to
      which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped
      and the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered to the
      facility.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a return to a foreign country of
    origin, the following information is required in Part A:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference number that is provided by the
      Minister for the movement document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the permit holder is the holder of the
      original import permit or permits, the name, telephone number,
      email address, mailing address and unique identification number of
      the foreign exporter that is named in the original import permit
      or permits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part A;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility from which the
      shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
      material is to be shipped for return;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address,
      mailing address and unique identification number of the holder of
      the export permit for the return;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the civic address of the facility in the country
      of origin to which the shipment is to be delivered;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shipping name and description that are set
        out in column 2 of Schedule 1 to the [Transportation of
        Dangerous Goods Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-286) and
        are associated with the UN number referred to in that line
        entry,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of the hazardous waste or
        hazardous recyclable material in kilograms or litres,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of containers used, if applicable,
        and the code 01 when the container is a drum, 02 when it is a
        tank, 03 when the hazardous waste or hazardous recyclable
        material is shipped in bulk, 04 when the container is a carton,
        05 when it is a bag, 06 when it is roll off or lugger, 07 when
        it is palletized, wrapped with plastic wrap and not in any other
        container, 08 when it is a mix of containers or 09 in any other
        case, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the applicable [[tariff
        item]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
        the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment is to be shipped
      and the date on which it is scheduled to be delivered to the
      facility.

#### [Movement Document --- Part B]{.HTitleText2} {#movement-document-part-b .SchedHeadL2}

- **2** The following information in Part B is required from an
  authorized carrier:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the authorized carrier's name, telephone number,
    email address, mailing address and unique identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual who
    signs Part B;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an authorized carrier that is to
    transport a shipment that contains hazardous waste or hazardous
    recyclable material as described in Part A into or out of Canada,
    the port and date of any entry into, and of any exit out of, Canada;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a confirmation

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the authorized carrier has received, and
      will transport, the shipment in accordance with the permit or
      permits, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, that they are to deliver the
      shipment to the receiving facility referred to in Part A of the
      movement document.

#### [Movement Document --- Part C]{.HTitleText2} {#movement-document-part-c .SchedHeadL2}

- - **3** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an import to or an export from
    Canada, or an export from, and import to, Canada following transit
    through a foreign country, the following information is required in
    Part C:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receiving facility's name, telephone number,
      email address, civic address and unique identification number;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part C;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment was delivered; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document, the quantity of the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, that was
      received.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a return to Canada or to a foreign
    country of origin, the following information is required in Part C:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, telephone number, email address, civic
      address and unique identification number of the facility to which
      the shipment was delivered;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and telephone number of the individual
      who signs Part C;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shipment was delivered; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each type of hazardous waste or hazardous
      recyclable material that is recorded as a line entry in the
      movement document, the quantity of the hazardous waste or
      hazardous recyclable material, in kilograms or litres, that was
      received.

class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Code</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Reason</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Production residues that are not
otherwise specified in this Part.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Off-specification products.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q3</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Products whose date for appropriate use
has expired.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q4</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Material that has been spilled or lost or
has undergone other mishap, including any material, equipment, etc.,
that has been contaminated as a result of the mishap.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q5</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Material that has been contaminated or
soiled as a result of planned actions (e.g., residues from cleaning
operations, packing material, containers).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q6</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Unusable parts (e.g., rejected batteries,
exhausted catalysts).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q7</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Substances that no longer perform
satisfactorily (e.g., contaminated acids, contaminated solvents,
exhausted tempering salts).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q8</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Residues from industrial processes (e.g.,
slags, still bottoms).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">9</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q9</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Residues from pollution abatement
processes (e.g., scrubber sludges, baghouse dusts, spent filters).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">10</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q10</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Machining or finishing residues (e.g.,
lathe turning, mill scales).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">11</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q11</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Residues from raw material processing
(e.g., mining residues, oil field slop).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">12</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q12</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Adulterated material (e.g., oils
contaminated with polychlorinated biphenyls).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">13</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q13</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any material, substance or product whose
use has been banned by law in the country of destination.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">14</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Q14</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Products for which there is no further
use (e.g., agricultural, household, office, commercial and shop
discards).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">15</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Q15</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Any material, substance or product that
results from remedial actions with respect to contaminated land.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">16</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Q16</td>
<td class="top">Any material, substance or product that the generator or
exporter declares to be waste and that are not referred to in this
Part.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>PART 1</p>
<p>Reasons Why Waste or Recyclable Material Is Intended for Disposal or
Recycling</p></figcaption>
</figure>

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">47</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">C47</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Substances of an explosive character
(Y15)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">48</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">C48</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Sulphur organic compounds</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">49</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">C49</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Any congenor of polychlorinated dibenzo-furan
(Y43)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">50</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">C50</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Any congenor of polychlorinated
dibenzo-p-dioxin (Y44)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">51</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">C51</td>
<td>Hydrocarbons and their oxygen, nitrogen and sulphur compounds that
are not otherwise taken into account in this Part</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>PART 3</p>
<p>Constituents of Potentially Hazardous Waste</p></figcaption>
</figure>

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21021-ID0EAAABABCA){#fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21021}If
  applicable, correspondence with codes referred to in Annex I to the
  Convention is indicated in brackets after the constituent.
  :::

<figure id="1287918">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Code<a
href="#fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21022"
id="fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21022-ID0EABABABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for PART 4 List of Hazardous
Characteristics</span><sup>b</sup></a></th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Hazardous
Characteristic</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H3</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Liquids, mixtures of liquids and liquids
containing solids in solution or suspension that give off a flammable
vapour at temperatures of not more than 60.5ºC, for a closed-cup test,
or not more than 65.6ºC for an open-cup test. (Since the results of
open- and closed-cup tests are not strictly comparable and even
individual results by the same test are often variable, regulations
varying from the above figures to make allowance for such differences
would be within the spirit of this description.) Includes paints,
varnishes, lacquers, etc., but does not include any substance or waste
that is otherwise classified on account of its dangerous
characteristics. (Flammable liquids)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H4.1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Solids or waste solids (other than those
classed as explosives) that, under conditions encountered in transport,
are readily combustible or may cause or contribute to fire through
friction. (Flammable solids)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H4.2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that is liable to
spontaneous heating under normal conditions encountered in transport or
to heating up in contact with air, and is liable to catch fire.
(Spontaneous combustion)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H4.3</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that is liable, by
interaction with water, to become spontaneously flammable or to give off
flammable gases in dangerous quantities. (In contact with water,
spontaneous combustion or emission of flammable gases)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H5.1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that is not
necessarily combustible itself but that may, generally by yielding
oxygen, cause or contribute to the combustion of other material.
(Oxidizing)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H5.2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any organic substance or waste containing
the bivalent-0-0-structure that may undergo exothermic self-accelerating
decomposition due to its thermal instability. (Organic peroxides)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H6.1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that is liable to
cause death, serious injury or harm to human health if swallowed,
inhaled or placed in contact with skin. (Poisonous (acute))</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H6.2</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste containing viable
micro-organisms or their toxins that are known or suspected to cause
disease in animals or humans. (Infectious Substances)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">9</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H8</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that, by chemical
action, causes severe damage when in contact with living tissue or, in
the case of leakage, materially damages or destroys other goods or the
means of transport. It may also cause other hazards. (Corrosives)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">10</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H10</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that is liable, by
interaction with air or water, to give off toxic gases in dangerous
quantities. (Liberation of toxic gases in contact with air or
water)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">11</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H11</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that, if it is
inhaled or ingested, or if it penetrates the skin, may involve delayed
or chronic effects, including carcinogenicity. (Toxic (delayed or
chronic))</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">12</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">H12</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Any substance or waste that, if it is
released, presents or may cause immediate or delayed adverse impacts to
the environment by means of bioaccumulation or has toxic effects on
biotic systems. (Ecotoxic)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">13</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">H13</td>
<td class="top">Any substance or waste that is capable, by any means
after disposal, of yielding another material (e.g., leachate that
possesses any of the characteristics listed in this Part).
(Leachate)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>PART 4</p>
<p>List of Hazardous Characteristics</p></figcaption>
</figure>

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21022-ID0EABABABBA){#fn_81000-2-1177-E01112017_hq_21022}Code
  numbers correspond to the hazard class numbering system in the United
  Nations Recommendations on the Transport of Dangerous Goods for H3
  through H8.
  :::

## [SCHEDULE 12]{.scheduleLabel}[(Clause 78(1)(a)(iii)(I) and Schedules 3 and 4)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1287933 .scheduleLabel}

<figure id="1287934">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Code</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Description of Hazardous
Waste and Hazardous Recyclable Material</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F001</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">The following spent halogenated solvents used
in degreasing: tetrachloroethylene, trichloroethylene, methylene
chloride, 1,1,1 trichloroethane, carbon tetrachloride and chlorinated
fluorocarbons; all spent solvent mixtures and blends used in degreasing
containing, before use, a total of 10% or more (by volume) of one or
more of the above halogenated solvents or those solvents listed as F002,
F004 or F005; and still bottoms from the recovery of those spent
solvents and spent solvent mixtures.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F002</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">The following spent halogenated solvents:
tetrachloroethylene, methylene chloride, trichloroethylene, 1,1,1
trichloroethane, chlorobenzene, 1,1,2 trichloro 1,2,2 trifluoroethane,
ortho dichlorobenzene, trichlorofluoromethane and 1,1,2-trichloroethane;
all spent solvent mixtures and blends containing, before use, a total of
10% or more (by volume) of one or more of the above halogenated solvents
or those listed as F001, F004 or F005; and still bottoms from the
recovery of those spent solvents and spent solvent mixtures.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F003</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">The following spent non-halogenated solvents:
xylenes, acetone, ethyl acetate, ethyl benzene, ethyl ether, methyl
isobutyl ketone, n butyl alcohol, cyclohexanone and methanol; all spent
solvent mixtures and blends containing, before use, only the above spent
non-halogenated solvents; and all spent solvent mixtures and blends
containing, before use, one or more of the above spent non-halogenated
solvents, and a total of 10% or more (by volume) of one or more of those
solvents listed as F001, F002, F004 or F005; and still bottoms from the
recovery of those spent solvents and spent solvent mixtures.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F004</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">The following spent non-halogenated solvents:
cresols, cresylic acid and nitrobenzene; all spent solvent mixtures and
blends containing, before use, a total of 10% or more (by volume) of one
or more of the above spent non-halogenated solvents or those solvents
listed as F001, F002 or F005; and still bottoms from the recovery of
those spent solvents and spent solvent mixtures.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F005</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">The following spent non-halogenated solvents:
toluene, methyl ethyl ketone, carbon disulphide, isobutanol, pyridine,
benzene, 2-ethoxyethanol and 2-nitropropane; all spent solvent mixtures
and blends containing, before use, a total of 10% or more (by volume) of
one or more of the above spent non-halogenated solvents or those
solvents listed as F001, F002 or F004; and still bottoms from the
recovery of those spent solvents and spent solvent mixtures.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F006</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Wastewater treatment sludges from
electroplating operations except for the following processes: (1)
sulphuric acid anodizing of aluminum; (2) tin plating on carbon steel;
(3) zinc plating (on a segregated basis) on carbon steel; (4) aluminum
or aluminum zinc plating on carbon steel; (5) cleaning or stripping
associated with tin, zinc or aluminum plating on carbon steel; and (6)
chemical etching and milling of aluminum.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F007</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Spent cyanide plating bath solutions from
electroplating operations.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F008</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Plating bath sludge from the bottom of
plating baths from electroplating operations if cyanides are used in the
process.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">9</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F009</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Spent stripping and cleaning bath solutions
from electroplating operations if cyanides are used in the process.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">10</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F010</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Quenching bath sludge from oil baths from
metal heat treating operations if cyanides are used in the process.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">11</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F011</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Spent cyanide solutions from salt bath pot
cleaning from metal heat treating operations.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">12</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F012</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Quenching wastewater treatment sludge from
metal heat treating operations if cyanides are used in the process.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">13</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F019</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Wastewater treatment sludge from the chemical
conversion coating of aluminum except from zirconium phosphating in
aluminum can washing if the phosphating is an exclusive conversion
coating process.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">14</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F020</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste from the production or manufacturing
use (as a reactant, chemical intermediate or component in a formulating
process) of tri- or tetrachlorophenol or of intermediates used to
produce their pesticide derivatives, excluding wastewater and spent
carbon from hydrogen chloride purification and waste from the production
of hexachlorophene from highly purified 2,4,5-trichlorophenol.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">15</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F021</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste from the production or manufacturing
use (as a reactant, chemical intermediate or component in a formulating
process) of pentachlorophenol or of intermediates used to produce its
derivatives, excluding wastewater and spent carbon from hydrogen
chloride purification.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">16</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F022</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste from the manufacturing use (as a
reactant, chemical intermediate or component in a formulating process)
of tetra-, penta- or hexachlorobenzenes under alkaline conditions,
excluding wastewater and spent carbon from hydrogen chloride
purification.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">17</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F023</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste from the production of material on
equipment previously used for the production or manufacturing use (as a
reactant, chemical intermediate or component in a formulating process)
of tri- and tetrachlorophenols, excluding wastewater and spent carbon
from hydrogen chloride purification and waste from equipment used only
for the production or use of hexachlorophene from highly purified
2,4,5-trichlorophenol.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">18</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F024</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Process waste, including distillation
residues, heavy ends, tars and reactor clean-out waste from the
production of certain chlorinated aliphatic hydrocarbons by free radical
catalyzed processes, the chlorinated aliphatic hydrocarbons being those
having carbon chain lengths ranging from 1 to 5, with varying amounts
and positions of chlorine substitution, and excluding wastewaters,
wastewater treatment sludge, spent catalysts and waste set out in this
Schedule.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">19</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F025</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Condensed light ends, spent filters and
filter aids, and spent desiccant waste from the production of certain
chlorinated aliphatic hydrocarbons, by free radical catalyzed processes,
the chlorinated aliphatic hydrocarbons being those having carbon chain
lengths ranging from 1 to 5, with varying amounts and positions of
chlorine substitution.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">20</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F026</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Waste from the production of material on
equipment previously used for the manufacturing use (as a reactant,
chemical intermediate or component in a formulating process) of tetra-,
penta- or hexachlorobenzene under alkaline conditions, excluding
wastewater and spent carbon from hydrogen chloride purification.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">21</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F027</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Discarded unused formulations containing
tri-, tetra- or pentachlorophenol or discarded unused formulations
containing compounds derived from those chlorophenols, excluding
formulations containing hexachlorophene synthesized from prepurified
2,4,5-trichlorophenol as the sole component.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">22</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F028</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Residues resulting from incineration or
treatment of soil contaminated with waste listed as F020, F021, F022,
F023, F026 or F027.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">23</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F032</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Wastewaters, spent formulations from wood
preserving processes generated at plants that currently use or have
previously used chlorophenolic formulations, process residuals and
preservative drippage, except wastewaters that have not come into
contact with process contaminants, spent formulations that potentially
cross-contaminated waste from wood preserving processes at plants that
do not resume or initiate use of chlorophenolic preservatives, and
bottom sediment sludge listed in Part 2 as K001.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">24</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F034</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Wastewaters, process residuals, preservative
drippage and spent formulations from wood preserving processes generated
at plants that use creosote formulations, excluding bottom sediment
sludge listed in Part 2 as K001 and wastewaters that have not come into
contact with process contaminants.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">25</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F035</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Wastewaters, process residuals, preservative
drippage and spent formulations from wood preserving processes generated
at plants that use inorganic preservatives containing arsenic or
chromium, excluding bottom sediment sludge listed in Part 2 as K001 and
wastewaters that have not come into contact with process
contaminants.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">26</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F037</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Petroleum refinery primary oil, water and
solids separation sludge; sludge generated from the gravitational
separation of oil, water and solids during the storage or treatment of
process wastewaters and oil cooling wastewaters from petroleum
refineries, including those generated in oil, water and solids
separators, tanks and impoundments, ditches and other conveyances, sumps
and stormwater units receiving dry weather flow; sludge generated in
stormwater units that do not receive dry weather flow; sludge generated
from non-contact once-through cooling waters segregated for treatment
from other processes or oily cooling waters; sludge generated in
biological treatment units that employ one of the following treatment
methods: activated sludge, trickling filter, rotating biological
contactor for the continuous accelerated biological oxidation of
wastewaters, or high-rate aeration (including sludge generated in one or
more additional units after wastewaters have been treated in biological
treatment units). Waste listed in Part 2 as K051 is excluded.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">27</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">F038</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Petroleum refinery secondary (emulsified)
oil, water and solids separation sludge; sludge or float generated from
the physical or chemical separation of oil, water and solids in process
wastewaters and oily cooling wastewaters from petroleum refineries,
including sludge and floats generated in induced air flotation (IAF)
units, tanks and impoundments, and in dissolved air flotation (DAF)
units; sludge generated in stormwater units that do not receive dry
weather flow; sludge generated from non-contact once-through cooling
waters segregated for treatment from other processes or oily cooling
water; sludge and float generated in biological treatment units that
employ one of the following treatment methods: activated sludge,
trickling filter, rotating biological contactor for the continuous
accelerated biological oxidation of wastewaters, or high-rate aeration
(including sludge and float generated in one or more additional units
after wastewaters have been treated in a biological treatment unit).
Waste listed as F037, or in Part 2 as K048 or K051, is excluded.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">28</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">F039</td>
<td>Leachate (liquids that percolated through land disposed waste)
resulting from the disposal of more than one waste classified as a
hazardous waste by being included in this Schedule.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h344">Residue
from the use of activated carbon for decolourization in the production
of veterinary pharmaceuticals from arsenic or organo arsenic
compounds.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3" id="t5703h354" class="noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h2 t5703h3 t5703h4 t5703h5">Ink formulation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h354">112</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h354">K086</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h354">Solvent
washes and sludge, caustic washes and sludge or water washes and sludge
from cleaning tubs and equipment used in the formulation of ink from
pigments, driers, soaps and stabilizers containing chromium and
lead.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3" id="t5703h358" class="noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h2 t5703h3 t5703h4 t5703h5">Coking</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">113</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K060</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Ammonia
still lime sludge from coking operations.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">114</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K087</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Decanter
tank tar sludge from coking operations.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">115</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K141</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Process
residues from the recovery of coal tar, including collecting sump
residues from the production of coke from coal and the recovery of coke
by-products produced from coal, excluding waste listed as K087.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">116</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K142</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Tar
storage tank residues from the production of coke from coal or from the
recovery of coke by-products produced from coal.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">117</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K143</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Process
residues from the recovery of light oil, including those generated in
stills, decanters and wash oil recovery units from the recovery of coke
by- products produced from coal.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">118</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K144</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Wastewater sump residues from light
oil refining, including intercepting or contamination sump sludge from
the recovery of coke by-products produced from coal.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">119</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K145</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Residues
from naphthalene collection and recovery operations from the recovery of
coke by-products produced from coal.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">120</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K147</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom" headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Tar
storage tank residues from coal tar refining.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight" headers="t5703h3 t5703h358">121</td>
<td class="noBorderRight" headers="t5703h1 t5703h4 t5703h358">K148</td>
<td headers="t5703h2 t5703h5 t5703h358">Residues from coal tar
distillation, including still bottoms.</td>
</tr>

</table>
